## 173. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF JUSTICE - HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 326001001

The Federal Ministry of Justice is principal legal adviser to Federal Government of Nigeria, responsible for legal policy formulation, prosecution of federal offences, law reform, administration of justice and international legal affairs. Headed by Attorney-General of the Federation and Minister of Justice (appointed by President as chief law officer of Federation under Constitution Section 150), the Ministry operates through Attorney-General's Chambers providing legal services to government. The Ministry supervises Legal Aid Council, Nigerian Law Reform Commission, National Agency for Prohibition of Trafficking in Persons (NAPTIP), Economic and Financial Crimes Commission (EFCC), Independent Corrupt Practices and Other Related Offences Commission (ICPC), Nigerian Correctional Service (formerly Prisons Service), Nigerian Copyright Commission, and Federal Public Complaints Commission (Ombudsman).

**Core Mandate:** To provide legal services to Federal Government, ensure administration of justice, prosecute federal offences, and uphold rule of law.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Serve as principal legal adviser to President and Federal Government
(b) Provide legal opinions and advice to Federal Ministries, Departments and Agencies
(c) Draft legislation, executive orders and legal instruments for government
(d) Represent Federal Government in litigation before all courts and tribunals
(e) Prosecute criminal offences under federal jurisdiction
(f) Oversee Economic and Financial Crimes Commission for anti-corruption enforcement
(g) Coordinate Independent Corrupt Practices Commission for corruption prevention
(h) Manage Nigerian Correctional Service for administration of correctional facilities
(i) Oversee Legal Aid Council providing legal services to indigent citizens
(j) Coordinate law reform through Nigerian Law Reform Commission
(k) Combat human trafficking through NAPTIP
(l) Manage international legal affairs and treaty negotiations
(m) Coordinate Nigeria's participation in international courts and tribunals
(n) Enforce intellectual property rights through Nigerian Copyright Commission
(o) Coordinate legal education and continuing legal education
(p) Manage federal government litigation portfolio
(q) Coordinate administration of criminal justice reforms
(r) Promote alternative dispute resolution mechanisms
(s) Manage extradition proceedings and mutual legal assistance
(t) Promote access to justice and legal empowerment

---

## 74. FEDERAL CAPITAL TERRITORY ADMINISTRATION (FCTA)
**MDA Code:** 437001001

The Federal Capital Territory Administration (FCTA) is the Nigerian ministry that administers the Federal Capital Territory (FCT), created following the Federal Capital Territory Act of 1976, which established the FCT as Nigeria's new federal capital. The FCTA was established on December 31, 2004, by President Olusegun Obasanjo following the scrapping of the Ministry of the Federal Capital Territory. The FCT is administered in a dual function of a Federal Ministry and a State Government, as Section 299 of the 1999 Constitution provides that the FCT is to be treated as if it were one of the states of the federation.

The FCTA is headed by a Minister appointed by the President, who exercises powers delegated by the President (who acts as the Governor of the FCT). The Permanent Secretary is the Accounting Officer and Chief Administrative Officer. Seven mandate secretariats were created: Education, Transport, Agriculture and Rural Development, Health and Human Services, Social Development, Legal Services, and Area Council Services. The Federal Capital Development Authority (FCDA) oversees physical infrastructure development.

The FCTA's mandate includes building and administering the Federal Capital Territory in compliance with the Abuja Master Plan; providing governance, infrastructure, and services to FCT residents; implementing policies for urban development, housing, and land administration; managing the six area councils (Abuja Municipal, Gwagwalada, Abaji, Kuje, Bwari, and Kwali); ensuring provision of utilities, transportation, and social services; maintaining security, environmental standards, and public health; enforcing building codes and development control; and managing the transition from Lagos to Abuja as Nigeria's capital. The FCTA operates through various departments and agencies to deliver municipal services while maintaining Abuja's status as one of Africa's best-planned cities.

---

## 177. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF NIGER DELTA HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 451001001

The Federal Ministry of Niger Delta Affairs was established in 2008 during President Umaru Yar'Adua's administration to address decades of neglect, environmental degradation, youth restiveness, militancy and underdevelopment in oil-rich Niger Delta region comprising nine oil-producing states: Abia, Akwa Ibom, Bayelsa, Cross River, Delta, Edo, Imo, Ondo and Rivers. Despite contributing over 80% of Nigeria's foreign exchange earnings, the region suffered environmental degradation from oil pollution and gas flaring, poverty, unemployment and insecurity. Led by Minister of Niger Delta Affairs assisted by Minister of State and Permanent Secretary, the Ministry coordinates special interventions complementing Niger Delta Development Commission (NDDC). The Ministry implements amnesty programmes for ex-militants, coordinates environmental remediation including Ogoniland cleanup, and facilitates peace building in oil-producing communities.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate development programmes and projects in Niger Delta region, facilitate peace building, environmental restoration and socio-economic development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate and implement special development policies for Niger Delta region
(b) Coordinate Federal Government interventions in Niger Delta complementing NDDC
(c) Facilitate infrastructure development including roads, bridges, electricity, water supply
(d) Coordinate environmental remediation and cleanup of oil-impacted sites
(e) Implement amnesty programmes for ex-militants and restive youths
(f) Promote youth empowerment and vocational training for Niger Delta youths
(g) Coordinate scholarships and educational support for Niger Delta students
(h) Facilitate conflict resolution and peace building in oil-producing communities
(i) Promote community development and grassroots empowerment
(j) Coordinate poverty alleviation interventions targeting Niger Delta poor
(k) Facilitate host community relations between oil companies and communities
(l) Address grievances regarding resource control and fiscal federalism
(m) Coordinate health interventions addressing health challenges in riverine areas
(n) Promote agricultural development and fisheries in Niger Delta
(o) Coordinate resettlement programmes for displaced communities
(p) Facilitate women empowerment programmes
(q) Coordinate maritime security in Niger Delta waters
(r) Monitor compliance with environmental standards by oil companies
(s) Coordinate disaster response in flood-prone riverine communities
(t) Facilitate economic diversification beyond oil in Niger Delta

---

## 186. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF WOMEN AFFAIRS - HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 514001001

The Federal Ministry of Women Affairs is responsible for formulating and implementing policies promoting gender equality, women empowerment, women's rights protection and mainstreaming gender in national development. Established to address gender disparities and promote women's advancement in Nigeria, the Ministry coordinates National Gender Policy implementation, combats gender-based violence, promotes women's economic empowerment and ensures gender mainstreaming across all sectors. The Ministry works closely with National Council of Women Societies and women's rights organizations.

**Core Mandate:** To promote gender equality, empower women, protect women's rights and ensure women's full participation in Nigeria's socio-economic and political development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate and implement national gender policies including National Gender Policy
(b) Promote gender mainstreaming across all government ministries, departments and agencies
(c) Coordinate women economic empowerment programmes and women entrepreneurship support
(d) Combat gender-based violence including domestic violence, sexual assault and harmful practices
(e) Promote women's political participation and representation in governance
(f) Coordinate implementation of international conventions including CEDAW (Convention on Elimination of All Forms of Discrimination Against Women)
(g) Facilitate women's access to education, healthcare, economic resources and justice
(h) Promote women's cooperatives and women's self-help groups
(i) Coordinate girl-child education and protection programmes
(j) Combat child marriage, female genital mutilation and other harmful traditional practices
(k) Promote women's inheritance rights and property ownership
(l) Coordinate widow protection programmes and vulnerable women support
(m) Facilitate women's access to credit and financial services
(n) Promote women's participation in science, technology, engineering and mathematics (STEM)
(o) Coordinate gender budgeting and gender-responsive planning
(p) Establish and maintain women development centers nationwide
(q) Promote work-life balance and maternity protection policies
(r) Coordinate gender data collection and gender statistics
(s) Facilitate partnership with civil society organizations on women's issues
(t) Coordinate special programmes for rural women and marginalized women groups

---

## 165. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF EDUCATION - HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 517001001

The Federal Ministry of Education is responsible for formulating, coordinating and monitoring national education policies, overseeing federal education institutions and ensuring quality education delivery at all levels. The Ministry provides policy direction for Nigeria's education sector encompassing basic education, secondary education, tertiary education and special education. Led by Minister of Education assisted by Ministers of State and Permanent Secretary, the Ministry supervises federal universities, polytechnics, colleges of education, unity schools (Federal Government Colleges) and specialized educational institutions while coordinating with state and local governments. Key regulatory agencies under the Ministry include National Universities Commission (NUC), National Board for Technical Education (NBTE), National Commission for Colleges of Education (NCCE), Universal Basic Education Commission (UBEC), National Commission for Nomadic Education (NCNE), and National Commission for Mass Literacy, Adult and Non-Formal Education.

**Core Mandate:** To formulate and implement policies ensuring qualitative and functional education for all Nigerians, coordinating education sector development at federal level.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate national education policy including National Policy on Education and ensure implementation
(b) Oversee federal universities, polytechnics and colleges of education
(c) Regulate establishment and accreditation of tertiary institutions through NUC, NBTE and NCCE
(d) Coordinate Unity Schools (Federal Government Colleges) nationwide
(e) Supervise Federal Science and Technical Colleges
(f) Coordinate nomadic education through National Commission for Nomadic Education
(g) Promote adult and non-formal education through National Commission for Mass Literacy
(h) Oversee special education for persons with disabilities
(i) Coordinate basic education programmes through Universal Basic Education Commission (UBEC)
(j) Regulate examinations and certifications through examination bodies
(k) Develop curriculum frameworks and approve curricula for federal institutions
(l) Ensure quality assurance in education through monitoring and evaluation
(m) Coordinate teacher education and professional development
(n) Manage scholarship programmes including Federal Government scholarships and bursaries
(o) Promote educational research and development
(p) Coordinate educational infrastructure development in federal institutions
(q) Facilitate access, equity and inclusion in education
(r) Promote girl-child education and gender equality
(s) Develop and implement educational technology and e-learning frameworks
(t) Coordinate education sector development plans and budgets

## 194. FEDERAL POLYTECHNIC ADO-EKITI

**MDA Code:** 517018001

The Federal Polytechnic Ado-Ekiti, Ekiti State, is a federal tertiary institution providing technical and vocational education at National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) levels. Established under supervision of Federal Ministry of Education and regulated by National Board for Technical Education (NBTE), the Polytechnic offers programmes in engineering, applied sciences, environmental studies, business studies and management. Located in Ado-Ekiti, Ekiti State capital, the institution contributes to middle-level manpower development for Nigeria's industrial and economic sectors.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality technical and vocational education producing technicians and technologists for Nigeria's industrial development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) programmes in NBTE-accredited fields
(b) Offer engineering technology programmes including electrical, mechanical, civil and computer engineering
(c) Provide applied science programmes including science laboratory technology and statistics
(d) Offer business and management programmes including accountancy, business administration and banking
(e) Provide environmental studies programmes including building technology, surveying and urban planning
(f) Conduct Students Industrial Work Experience Scheme (SIWES) for practical training
(g) Maintain workshops, laboratories and studios for hands-on technical training
(h) Award National Diploma certificates for two-year programmes after secondary school
(i) Award Higher National Diploma certificates for two-year advanced programmes after ND
(j) Conduct examinations and continuous assessments as prescribed by NBTE
(k) Provide consultancy services to industries and government agencies
(l) Conduct applied research addressing technological and industrial challenges
(m) Offer short courses and continuing education for professionals
(n) Collaborate with industries for curriculum development and graduate placement
(o) Maintain entrepreneurship development center for student business incubation
(p) Provide computer literacy and ICT skills training
(q) Operate Federal Polytechnic admission through JAMB and institutional screening
(r) Maintain academic standards through internal and NBTE quality assurance
(s) Provide students' welfare services including accommodation and health services
(t) Contribute to community development through technology transfer and outreach

(t) Engage in community development through technology transfer and outreach programmes

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 201-220

## 201. FEDERAL POLYTECHNIC DAMATURU

**MDA Code:** 517018015

The Federal Polytechnic Damaturu, Yobe State, is a federal tertiary institution providing technical and vocational education in North-East Nigeria. Located in Damaturu, Yobe State capital, the Polytechnic serves a region significantly affected by Boko Haram insurgency, making technical education crucial for reconstruction and youth engagement. Established to expand access to polytechnic education in Yobe State and provide alternative to university education, the institution addresses middle-level manpower needs for the state's agricultural, commercial and emerging industrial sectors. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education and NBTE regulation, the Polytechnic contributes to post-conflict reconstruction through skills development.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality technical and vocational education producing skilled technicians and technologists for Yobe State and North-East Nigeria's development and reconstruction.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide National Diploma and Higher National Diploma programmes in NBTE-accredited disciplines
(b) Offer engineering technology programmes including electrical, mechanical, civil and computer engineering
(c) Provide agricultural technology programmes supporting Yobe's predominantly agricultural economy
(d) Offer business studies programmes including accountancy, business administration, banking and office management
(e) Provide applied science programmes including mathematics, statistics, computer science and science laboratory technology
(f) Offer environmental studies programmes including building technology, surveying and architecture
(g) Conduct Students Industrial Work Experience Scheme for practical industry training
(h) Maintain technical workshops, laboratories and agricultural demonstration facilities
(i) Award National Diploma certificates for two-year post-secondary programmes
(j) Award Higher National Diploma certificates for two-year advanced programmes beyond ND
(k) Conduct examinations and continuous assessments following NBTE guidelines
(l) Provide technical consultancy to government agencies, businesses and farmers
(m) Conduct applied research addressing regional development and reconstruction challenges
(n) Offer continuing education and skills upgrading programmes for post-conflict youth
(o) Establish entrepreneurship development programs for graduate self-employment
(p) Provide ICT training and digital literacy programmes
(q) Collaborate with reconstruction agencies for student training and employment
(r) Maintain quality assurance through NBTE accreditation compliance
(s) Provide student welfare including security, accommodation and health services
(t) Engage in peacebuilding and community development through educational outreach

Monguno experienced significant conflict impact including occupation by insurgents. Establishing federal polytechnic in Monguno represents federal government's commitment to post-conflict reconstruction and providing educational opportunities for youth as counter-insurgency strategy. (e) Develop applied science programmes including computer science
(f) Establish business and management programmes for economic revitalization
(g) Conduct Students Industrial Work Experience Scheme with reconstruction agencies
(h) Develop workshops, laboratories and teaching facilities despite security challenges
(i) Recruit qualified staff willing to serve in post-conflict environment
(j) Seek NBTE accreditation for programmes
(k) Develop institutional infrastructure with security considerations
(l) Establish admission procedures attracting conflict-affected youth
(m) Conduct examinations and assessments following NBTE guidelines
(n) Provide skills training for ex-combatants and displaced persons
(o) Establish consultancy supporting reconstruction efforts
(p) Create entrepreneurship programs for livelihood development
(q) Develop ICT infrastructure and training
(r) Provide psychosocial support and counseling for trauma-affected students
(s) Ensure enhanced security measures for staff and students
(t) Engage in peacebuilding and community reconciliation through education

(q) Create entrepreneurship programs for agribusiness startups
(r) Develop ICT infrastructure for precision agriculture training
(s) Provide student welfare and security given farmer-herder conflicts
(t) Engage in agricultural extension and food security initiatives

## 222. FEDERAL POLYTECHNIC, OROGUN, DELTA STATE

**MDA Code:** 517018034

The Federal Polytechnic Orogun, Delta State, is a newly established federal polytechnic located in Orogun, Ughelli North Local Government Area of Delta State. Delta State is major oil-producing state in Niger Delta with significant petroleum industry presence. Orogun area has oil and gas operations providing context for petroleum-related technical education. The Polytechnic addresses technical manpower needs for oil and gas sector while providing educational opportunities for host communities. As new institution, the Polytechnic is building capacity and seeking NBTE accreditation.

**Core Mandate:** To provide technical and vocational education producing technicians and technologists particularly for oil, gas and related industries in Niger Delta.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide National Diploma and Higher National Diploma programmes upon NBTE accreditation
(b) Develop petroleum engineering technology programmes supporting oil and gas sector
(c) Offer engineering programmes including electrical, mechanical, chemical and civil engineering technology
(d) Establish applied science programmes including chemistry, physics and environmental science
(e) Develop business studies programmes including petroleum business management
(f) Offer environmental studies programmes including environmental management and surveying
(g) Conduct Students Industrial Work Experience Scheme with oil companies
(h) Develop workshops, laboratories and specialized petroleum equipment
(i) Recruit qualified staff with petroleum industry experience where relevant
(j) Seek NBTE accreditation for all programmes
(k) Develop institutional infrastructure with industry-standard facilities
(l) Establish admission procedures attracting students interested in petroleum sector
(m) Conduct examinations and assessments following NBTE guidelines
(n) Develop partnerships with oil companies for training and equipment
(o) Establish consultancy services for petroleum operators
(p) Create entrepreneurship programs for oil and gas services sector
(q) Develop ICT infrastructure and training
(r) Provide student welfare services
(s) Promote environmental sustainability in petroleum operations
(t) Engage with host community on oil industry benefits and community development

The Federal Polytechnic Kabo, Kano State, is a newly established federal polytechnic located in Kabo, Kabo Local Government Area of Kano State. Established to expand access to technical education in Kano State which already has Federal Polytechnic Kaduna nearby, the institution represents federal government's commitment to educational expansion. Kano as Nigeria's second-largest city and major commercial center requires substantial technical manpower for industries, construction and commerce. As new institution, the Polytechnic is developing infrastructure, recruiting staff and seeking NBTE accreditation.

**Core Mandate:** To provide technical and vocational education producing middle-level manpower for industrial and economic development of Kano State and North-West Nigeria.

(b) Develop engineering technology programmes relevant to Kano's industrial base

**MDA Code:** 517018036

The Federal Polytechnic Ngodo Isuochi, Abia State, is a newly established federal polytechnic located in Ngodo, Isuochi, Umunneochi Local Government Area of Abia State. Abia State in South-East Nigeria is known for commerce, trading and small-scale manufacturing. Isuochi area is rural compared to Aba (commercial capital) and Umuahia (state capital), making polytechnic establishment significant for rural development. The institution represents federal presence in underserved rural area. As new institution, the Polytechnic is developing infrastructure, recruiting staff and seeking NBTE accreditation.

**Core Mandate:** To provide technical and vocational education producing middle-level manpower for industrial and economic development with focus on rural industrialization.

**Statutory Functions:**

(c) Establish agricultural technology programmes for rural farming development

(e) Develop business and management programmes for entrepreneurship and trading

## 89. Federal College of Education, Abeokuta
**MDA Code:** 517019001

Federal College of Education, Abeokuta was established in 1976 as the Federal Advanced Teachers College, one of four similar colleges alongside those at Okene, Pankshin, and Yola. It is the first tertiary institution in Ogun State. The college started at the then Abeokuta Grammar School, Isale-Igbein, in the centre of Abeokuta, the capital of Ogun State. The foundation day is January 27, 1977, when the first set of 86 students were registered.

The college moved to its permanent site in 1983 at Osiele along the Abeokuta-Ibadan Road, occupying a land area of 632.95 hectares vested by the Federal Government via Gazette No. 4164 of August 25, 1977. In 2010, the Centre for Degree Programmes (CEDEP) was established following resource assessment by the University of Ibadan and National Universities Commission.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college's primary mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities, and management; and to carry out research in the development and adaptation of techniques. Core functions include: offering three-year full-time and five-year sandwich courses leading to the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE); producing highly motivated and skilled teachers for Primary, Junior Secondary, and Senior Secondary Schools; providing degree programmes in affiliation with the University of Ibadan and Lagos State University; offering Post Graduate Diploma in Education (PGDE); and providing an all-round total quality teaching, research, and public service environment.

---

## 90. Federal College of Education (Technical), Akoka
**MDA Code:** 517019002

The Federal College of Education (Technical), Akoka (formerly National Technical Teachers College) was established in 1967 by the Federal Government of Nigeria following the Gailers' Report of August 1964 titled "A National Plan for the Development of Technical Education in the Federal Republic of Nigeria." The institution was the first Technical Teacher Education institution in the country, initially awarding Technical Teachers' Certificate (TTC) for professionals in commercial and technical education.

In 1982, the college was renamed Federal College of Education (Technical), Akoka, due to expansion in curricula and courses. The college became a fully autonomous academic institution in 1986 with the promulgation of the Federal College of Education Decree No. 4 of 1986 (as amended in 1993). The college moved to its permanent site along St. Finbarr's College Road, Akoka, in September 1975. It is approved by the National Universities Commission and affiliated with Federal University of Technology, Minna, and University of Benin for degree programmes.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is grooming and producing teachers in technical, vocational, and commercial academic disciplines. Core functions include: producing self-reliant Technical and Vocational Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) graduates grounded in Information and Communication Technology, research, and teaching; preparing students to instruct technical subjects at elementary, secondary, and professional levels; offering NCE programmes in Technical Education, Vocational Education, Science Education, and Business Education; awarding undergraduate degrees through university affiliations; and contributing to the technological and socio-economic development of Nigeria through effective research for sustainable living.

---

## 91. Federal College of Education (Technical), Asaba
**MDA Code:** 517019003

Federal College of Education (Technical), Asaba is a federal government higher education institution located in Asaba City, Delta State. Decree No. 4, published in the Federal Republic of Nigeria Extraordinary Official Gazette No. 16 Vol. 73 of March 21, 1986, gave birth to the institution. The college began operations in 1987 at the former Asaba Technical College (ATC) as its temporary site. Asaba is a rapidly developing urban centre and administrative headquarters of Delta State.

The college has relocated to its permanent site along Asaba/Ibusa Road. It is affiliated with the University of Benin for degree programmes and has been approved for independent degree operations under the Federal Colleges of Education Act 2023.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The basic mission of the college is to improve the quality of Technical Education in Nigeria. Core functions include: training professional teachers in technical, vocational, and commercial academic areas; offering pre-degree, NCE, Professional Diploma in Education (PDE), and degree programmes; running competitive integrated foundation year programmes for students who do not meet standard entry criteria; providing world-class learning facilities with well-equipped laboratories, modern equipment, and innovative technologies; preparing students for the education sector; and fostering excellence in learning through multi-media classrooms and modern libraries. The college comprises the School of Education, School of Business Education, School of Technical Education, School of Vocational Education, and School of Computer and Science Education.

---

## 92. Federal College of Education (Technical), Bichi
**MDA Code:** 517019004

Federal College of Education (Technical), Bichi is a federal government higher education institution located in Bichi, Kano State. The college was established by Decree No. 4 of March 14, 1986, and reviewed by Decree No. 6 of January 1993. The institution was established in 1986 and commenced full operation in 1987 with the aim of producing qualified, technical and vocational-oriented teachers.

The college is affiliated with Abubakar Tafawa Balewa University for its degree programmes. It offers relevant courses and programmes at NCE and degree levels and provides vocational/technical services. The current Provost is Bashir Muhammad Fagge. The college has been approved for independent degree operations under the new Federal Colleges of Education Act 2023.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is to produce specialist teachers in technical, vocational, business, and science subjects for the 9-3-4 system of education. Core functions include: running Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) programmes in Technical, Vocational, Business, Science, and Primary Education Studies; producing qualified and dynamic teachers who can successfully perform teacher roles as enshrined in the National Policy on Education; fitting graduates into the world of work; offering degree programmes in chosen lines of endeavour; ensuring effective and qualitative training of teachers for basic education and rapid technological development of the nation; and providing vocational/technical services to the community.

---

## 96. Federal College of Education (Technical), Gombe
**MDA Code:** 517019005

Federal College of Education (Technical), Gombe is a federal government higher education institution located in Gombe State. The college was established in 1977, originally known as National Technical Teachers College before being renamed Federal College of Education (Technical), Gombe. It started at a temporary site in the former Gombe Crafts School under a principal's leadership before moving to its permanent site.

The college is affiliated with Abubakar Tafawa Balewa University and the University of Maiduguri for degree programmes. The college has been approved for independent degree operations under the Federal Colleges of Education Act 2023. The current Provost is Dr. Aliyu Adamu, who also serves as Chairman of the Committee of Federal Colleges of Education in Nigeria.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate since inception has been producing technical, vocational, and science teachers for primary and secondary schools. Core functions include: preparing students to acquire Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) over a three-year programme; providing full-time courses in sciences, vocational education, arts, social sciences, and professional preparation of teachers; organising workshops, seminars, conferences, and in-service courses to update practicing teachers' knowledge; striving to be a Centre of Excellence producing highly qualified, competent, and motivated Science, Vocational, and Technical teachers for Nigeria's basic education levels; and cultivating a vibrant learning environment encouraging creativity, innovation, and collaboration. The college comprises six schools: Education, Vocational Education, Business Education, Technical Education, Science Education, and Primary Education Studies.

---

## 97. Federal College of Education (Technical), Gusau
**MDA Code:** 517019006

Federal College of Education (Technical), Gusau is a federal-owned conventional degree and NCE awarding institution located in Gusau, the capital of Zamfara State. The college was officially established in 1989 under the Federal College of Education Decree 1989 (as amended in 1993), and academic activities commenced in May 1990 with an initial intake of 110 students in the School of Business Education and School of Industrial Education.

The college has a unique distinction as the only institution in Africa specifically established for training women technical teachers. It was founded to encourage women to take up technical and vocational subjects for the Nigeria Certificate in Education. The college has graduated over 5,000 women NCE graduates since establishment, with current female-only student enrolment exceeding 7,000. It is affiliated with Federal University Gusau for degree programmes.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is to train and graduate competent female technology teachers and potential entrepreneurs for technological and national development. Core functions include: producing specialist teachers in technical, vocational, business, and science subjects for the education system; encouraging women to participate in technical and vocational fields; offering NCE programmes in Electrical/Electronics Technology, Computer Science, Fine and Applied Arts, Home Economics, Physical and Health Education, and other technical subjects; providing professional diploma programmes; and supporting the training of female technical educators for Nigeria's development needs. The college offers both NCE and degree programmes under its dual mandate.

---

## 104. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION KATSINA

**MDA Code:** 517019008

The Federal College of Education Katsina, formerly Federal Advanced Teachers' College Katsina, was founded in 1976 as one of Nigeria's pioneering teacher training institutions. Established under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004 (originally Decree No. 4 of March 14, 1986, amended by Decree 6 of January 1, 1993), the college's core mandate is to produce well-qualified holders of the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) to teach in primary and junior secondary schools across Nigeria. Operating initially on two campuses (U.P.E. Teachers' College Dutsin-ma for science students and Katsina town for Arts, Humanities and Vocational Education students), operations were consolidated in 1981 at the permanent site along Katsina-Dutsinma road. Specific functions include: (a) to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of educational techniques; (c) to establish and maintain such schools and other teaching units within the college as the Council may determine; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in and scope and requirements of education; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain such educational, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff as the Council considers necessary; (h) to hold and manage property; and (i) to perform such other acts, whether or not incidental to the foregoing, as may advance the college's objectives.

---

## 105. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION OBUDU

**MDA Code:** 517019010

The Federal College of Education Obudu is a federal tertiary institution located in Obudu, Cross River State, Nigeria, established under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The college operates as a body corporate with perpetual succession empowered to sue and be sued in its corporate name. The institution's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, and to carry out research in the development and adaptation of educational techniques. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) for deployment to primary and secondary schools across Nigeria; (b) to conduct research in development and adaptation of educational techniques and methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units, schools, or extramural departments within the college or outside as the Council may determine; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order of Council; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles, distinctions, awards and forms of assistance; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in and scope and requirements of education; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain such educational, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff as the Council considers necessary or desirable; (h) to hold and manage any property movable or immovable; and (i) to do such acts and things whether or not incidental to the foregoing powers as may advance the objectives of the college. Governance vests in the Governing Council, operating under Federal Ministry of Education supervision through the National Commission for Colleges of Education (NCCE).

---

## 108. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION OKENE

**MDA Code:** 517019011

The Federal College of Education Okene is a federal government higher education institution located in Okene, Kogi State, established in 1974 as Federal Advanced Teachers College, Okene, and renamed Federal College of Education, Okene in 1985. Operating under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004 (originally Decree No. 4 of March 14, 1986, amended by Decree 6 of January 1, 1993), the college is one of Nigeria's pioneer teacher training institutions alongside Abeokuta, Pankshin and Yola colleges. The college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, and to carry out research in development and adaptation of educational techniques. Under the Federal Colleges of Education (Amendment) Act 2023 providing for "dual mandate," the college has been granted approval to award degree programmes autonomously without university affiliation. Specific functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) for deployment to primary and secondary schools; (b) to conduct research in development and adaptation of educational techniques; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units or extramural departments; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and academic titles; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays fostering appreciation of educational trends and requirements; (g) to award degrees alongside NCE programmes under the dual mandate provision; (h) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities; (i) to hold and manage property; and (j) to perform other acts that may advance the college's objectives. Supervised by Federal Ministry of Education through National Commission for Colleges of Education.

---

## 109. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION OMUKU

**MDA Code:** 517019012

The Federal College of Education (Technical) Omoku was established in 1989 and is located in Omoku, Ogba/Egbema/Ndoni Local Government Area, Rivers State, South-South Nigeria. Operating under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004, specifically listed in the First Schedule as "Federal College of Education (Technical), Omoku, Rivers State," the college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, with particular emphasis on technical education. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified technical teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) with technical specialization for deployment to primary and junior secondary schools, particularly for technical and vocational education; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of technical education techniques and methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain technical schools and other teaching units within the college as the Governing Council may determine; (d) to establish further technical schools within the college by order of Council; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles and distinctions; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in technical and general education, including vocational training requirements; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational facilities including technical workshops, laboratories, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff; (h) to hold and manage property both movable and immovable; and (i) to do such acts and things whether or not incidental to the foregoing powers as may advance the objectives of the college. Under the Federal Colleges of Education (Amendment) Act 2023, the college may award degree programmes alongside NCE under the dual mandate provision.

---

## 110. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION OYO

**MDA Code:** 517019014

The Federal College of Education (Special), Oyo, was established on October 5, 1977, as Federal Advanced Teachers College (Special) and is the only specialized college of its kind in Nigeria and Sub-Saharan Africa dedicated to special education. Operating under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004, specifically listed in the First Schedule as "Federal College of Education (Special), Oyo Town, Oyo State," the college was upgraded from Federal Advanced Teachers College to Federal College of Education with mandate to award National Certificate of Education (NCE) in 1977 during General Olusegun Obasanjo's military administration. The college's unique mandate is to train teachers and para-professionals at the NCE level who, upon graduation, can handle both normal and special needs pupils/students in any classroom situation in primary and lower secondary education. Specific functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers in special education holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) specializing in teaching students with various disabilities including visual impairment, hearing impairment, intellectual disabilities, physical disabilities, and learning difficulties; (b) to train para-professionals in special education fields; (c) to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, with special education integration; (d) to carry out research in development and adaptation of special education techniques and inclusive education methodologies; (e) to establish and maintain specialized teaching units and facilities for students with disabilities; (f) to provide specialized facilities for teaching and training teachers of the handicapped; (g) to accommodate and educate persons with disabilities; (h) to mount exhibitions fostering appreciation of special education needs; and (i) to perform acts advancing special education objectives. According to UNDP/UNESCO 1996 report (NIR/87/008), the college has the best qualified staff in Special Education in Nigeria and West, North, East and Central Africa.

---

## 111. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION POTISKUM

**MDA Code:** 517019016

The Federal College of Education (Technical) Potiskum was established in 1991 and is located in Potiskum, Yobe State, North-East Nigeria. Operating under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004, specifically listed in the First Schedule as "Federal College of Education (Technical), Potiskum, Borno State" (subsequently transferred to Yobe State created in 1991), the college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, with emphasis on technical and vocational education. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified technical teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) with technical specialization for deployment to primary and junior secondary schools, particularly for technical and vocational subjects; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of technical education techniques, methodologies and vocational training approaches; (c) to establish and maintain technical schools and other teaching units within the college as the Governing Council may determine; (d) to establish further technical schools within the college by order of Council; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles and distinctions; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in technical education, vocational training scope and requirements; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational facilities including technical workshops, laboratories, vocational training centers, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff; (h) to hold and manage property both movable and immovable; and (i) to do such acts and things whether or not incidental to the foregoing powers as may advance the objectives of the college. Under Federal Colleges of Education (Amendment) Act 2023 providing dual mandate, the college may award degree programmes alongside NCE qualifications. The college serves the educational needs of Yobe State and North-East Nigeria.

## 112. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION UMUNZE

**MDA Code:** 517019017

The Federal College of Education (Technical) Umunze was established by Decree No. 4 of March 14, 1986, and fully commenced operations in November 1989. Located in Umunze, Orumba South Local Government Area of Anambra State, South-East Nigeria, the college operates under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004, specifically listed in First Schedule as "Federal College of Education (Technical), Umunze, Anambra State." The college is affiliated to Nnamdi Azikiwe University, Awka for degree programmes. Pioneer leaders include late Igwe of Umunze, Mathias Ugochukwu and Igwe of Azia, Prof. T. I. Eze (first Provost). The college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, with emphasis on technical and vocational education. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified technical teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) with technical specialization for primary and junior secondary schools, particularly technical and vocational subjects; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of technical education techniques and vocational training methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain technical schools and teaching units within the college as Governing Council determines; (d) to establish further technical schools by order of Council; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and academic titles; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays fostering appreciation of technical education trends and requirements; (g) to award PRE-NCE, NCE (Regular, CEP and Sandwich), Degree (Regular, CEP and Sandwich) and Post Graduate Diploma in Education certificates; (h) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational facilities including technical workshops, laboratories, recreational and residential facilities; (i) to hold and manage property; and (j) to perform acts advancing the college's technical education objectives. Under Federal Colleges of Education (Amendment) Act 2023 dual mandate, the college awards degrees independently.

## 113. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION YOLA

**MDA Code:** 517019018

The Federal College of Education Yola was established in 1976 as Federal Advanced Teachers College, Yola, being one of Nigeria's four pioneer Federal Advanced Teachers Colleges alongside Abeokuta, Okene, and Pankshin. Located in Yola, Adamawa State, North-East Nigeria, the college operates under Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004 (originally Decree No. 4 of March 14, 1986, amended by Decree 6 of January 1, 1993), specifically listed in First Schedule as "Federal College of Education Yola, Gongola State" (subsequently transferred to Adamawa State created in 1991). The college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) for deployment to primary and secondary schools across Nigeria; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of educational techniques and teaching methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units, schools, or extramural departments within the college as the Governing Council may determine; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles and distinctions; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in educational scope and requirements; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff; (h) to hold and manage property both movable and immovable; and (i) to perform such other acts and things whether or not incidental to the foregoing powers as may advance the objectives of the college. Under Federal Colleges of Education (Amendment) Act 2023 providing dual mandate, the college has been approved to award degree programmes independently without university affiliation, having previously operated affiliated degree programmes. The college serves Adamawa State and the wider North-East region.

---

## 93. Federal College of Education, Eha-Amufu
**MDA Code:** 517019020

Federal College of Education, Eha-Amufu is a federal government higher education institution located in Eha-Amufu, Enugu State. The college was established on February 21, 1981, by Chief Jim Ifeanyichukwu Nwobodo, the then Governor of old Anambra State. The Anambra State House of Assembly enacted Anambra State of Nigeria Law No. 28 of 1982, retroactively establishing the college effective from February 21, 1981.

The site was formerly occupied by the Federal Government-owned Teacher Training College (TTC), Eha-Amufu. Following state creation in 1991, Enugu State Government inherited the college. The Federal Government took over in April 1993 with financial effect from January 1994. Decree No. 4 of 1986, amended by Decree No. 34 of 1993 and Decree No. 6 of 1993, established the Federal College of Education, Eha-Amufu as the 20th Federal College of Education. It is affiliated with the University of Nigeria for degree programmes.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mission is to be a distinguished centre of academic excellence and innovation for pre-service and in-service teacher education as well as efficient and effective management. Core functions include: training students who make significant impact in Nigeria's educational sector; providing academic excellence, vocational education, and high moral training; offering NCE, Degree, and Professional Diploma in Education programmes; establishing a Directorate of Entrepreneurship to equip students with skills for self-reliance and job creation; and conducting research in teacher education. The college has received accreditation from NCCE for 17 academic programmes.

---

## 95. Federal College of Education, Gidan Madi
**MDA Code:** 517019022

Federal College of Education, Gidan Madi (FCE-GM) was established in 2019 by the Federal Government of Nigeria as one of six new Federal Colleges of Education approved by the administration of President Muhammadu Buhari. The college is located in the ancient city of Gidan Madi, Tangaza Local Government Area, Sokoto State. Its motto is "Learning for Self Reliance."

The college started academic activities in 2023 with eight programmes and matriculated 287 pioneer students during its maiden matriculation ceremony. Prof. Faruk Umar-Zaki serves as Provost. Plans are underway to expand programmes to complete the School of Vocational and Technical Education and School of Science Education.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is the production of teachers with intellectual and professional background adequate for their assignment and adaptable to changing situations in Nigeria and the world. Core functions include: providing a conducive environment with standard, modern facilities; engaging competent and skillful manpower; enrolling young people for practical training, intellectual development, and behavioural transformation in education delivery; adequately addressing educational sector needs of respective communities; training skilled, disciplined, and responsible educators committed to serving humanity; offering programmes in Metalwork Technology, Woodwork Technology, Electrical and Electronics, Computer Science, Business Education, Computer Education, Mathematics, Physics, and planned expansions in Automobile Technology, Building Technology, Agricultural Science, and Home Economics.

---

## 103. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION JAMA'ARE, BAUCHI STATE

**MDA Code:** 517019023

The Federal College of Education Jama'are, Bauchi State, is one of six Federal Colleges of Education established in May 2020 by President Muhammadu Buhari's administration, representing the North-East geo-political zone's allocation. Located in Jama'are, Bauchi State, the college operates under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The establishment was driven by Federal Government recognition that Bauchi, Benue, Ebonyi, Osun, Sokoto and Edo states lacked Federal Colleges of Education, representing deliberate policy intervention to address geographical imbalances and enhance teacher production capacity for Universal Basic Education implementation. The college's statutory mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management. Specific functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) capable of teaching in primary and junior secondary schools nationwide; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of educational techniques and methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units, schools, or extramural departments within the college as the Governing Council may determine; (d) to institute and award fellowships, scholarships, medals, prizes and other academic titles and distinctions; (e) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of educational trends, scope and requirements; (f) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities for staff and students; and (g) to perform other acts whether incidental or not that may advance the college's educational objectives as specified in the Act.

---

## 106. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION ODUGBO, BENUE STATE

**MDA Code:** 517019024

The Federal College of Education Odugbo, Benue State, is one of six Federal Colleges of Education established in May 2020 by President Muhammadu Buhari's administration to address teacher shortages in Nigeria's Universal Basic Education system. Located in Odugbo, Benue State, representing the North-Central geo-political zone's allocation, the college operates under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The establishment followed National Commission for Colleges of Education's identification that Bauchi, Benue, Ebonyi, Osun, Sokoto and Edo states lacked Federal Colleges of Education, representing deliberate policy intervention to ensure equitable distribution of educational infrastructure. The college's statutory mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management. Specific functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) capable of teaching in primary and junior secondary schools; (b) to carry out research in development and adaptation of educational techniques and methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units, schools, or extramural departments within the college as the Governing Council may determine; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles and distinctions; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of educational trends, scope and requirements; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities for staff and students; (h) to hold and manage property both movable and immovable; and (i) to perform other acts whether incidental or not that may advance the college's objectives as specified in the Federal Colleges of Education Act.

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 101-120

## 101. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION ISU, EBONYI STATE

**MDA Code:** 517019025

The Federal College of Education (Technical) Isu, Ebonyi State, is one of six Federal Colleges of Education established by the Federal Government in May 2020 under President Muhammadu Buhari's administration, distributed across Nigeria's six geo-political zones to address teacher shortages. Located in Isuokoma, Onicha Local Government Area of Ebonyi State, representing the South-East zone allocation, the college operates under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The institution's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management. Specific functions include: (a) to establish an academic institution where excellence and discipline in technology and learning produce professionally trained and skilled teachers with technical finesse; (b) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) capable of teaching in primary and junior secondary schools; (c) to establish and maintain such schools and other teaching units within the college as the Council may determine; (d) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles; (e) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in educational scope and requirements; (f) to carry out research in the development and adaptation of educational techniques; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities; and (h) to perform other acts that may advance the college's objectives. The college commenced academic operations in October 2021 under pioneer Provost Prof. Reuben Okechukwu.

---

## 102. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION IWO, OSUN STATE

**MDA Code:** 517019026

The Federal College of Education Iwo, Osun State, is one of six Federal Colleges of Education established on November 18, 2019, by President Muhammadu Buhari's administration, representing the South-West geo-political zone's allocation. Located in Iwo, Osun State, the college operates under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The institution was established to address critical shortage of qualified teachers needed for Universal Basic Education (UBE) delivery. The college's statutory mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management, and to carry out research in the development and adaptation of educational techniques. Specific functions include: (a) to produce well-qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) to teach in primary and junior secondary schools; (b) to establish and maintain teaching units within the college or extramural departments as may be determined by the Council; (c) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic distinctions; (d) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of educational trends, scope and requirements; (e) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff; (f) to conduct research in development and adaptation of teaching techniques and methodologies; and (g) to perform any other acts whether incidental to the foregoing powers that may advance the college's objectives. Principal officers were appointed March 16, 2021, with operations commencing April 2021. The college collaborates with UNICEF, DFID, NITDA, NCC, CBN, NTI, UBEC and SUBEBs.

---

## 94. Federal College of Education (Technical), Ekiadolor
**MDA Code:** 517019027

Federal College of Education (Technical), Ekiadolor (FCET Ekiadolor), formerly the College of Education, Ekiadolor, is located in Edo State, Southern Nigeria. The institution was originally established in 1980 under the administration of Professor Ambrose Folorunsho Alli of the defunct Bendel State.

In 2020, the Federal Government announced plans to establish new Federal Colleges of Education, including one using facilities of the defunct Tayo Akpata University of Education in Ekiadolor. In April 2022, the Edo State College of Education was handed over to the Federal Government and became the Federal College of Education (Technical), Ekiadolor. The college opened for students on December 2, 2022, with lectures starting December 19, 2022. Pioneer Principal Officers include Dr. Emmanuel Nkoro Asagha (Provost), Mr. Henry O. Itedjere (Registrar), Mr. Solomon Ediae (Bursar), and Dr. Tony Obaseki (College Librarian).

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is to establish a citadel of learning for Technical and Vocational Education Training (TVET). Core functions include: producing disciplined and qualified teachers for enhancement of Technical and Vocational Education Training; ensuring professionalism in Technical and Vocational Education in Nigeria; encouraging advancement of research in TVET among teachers; providing high-quality training empowering students with relevant skills and attitudes in advancing technical and entrepreneurial knowledge; fostering innovation and lifelong learning culture; awarding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) to graduates; augmenting the shortfall of teachers; and meeting learning, research, and instructional needs of host communities and Nigeria as a whole.

---

## 88. Federal College of Education (Technical), Yauri
**MDA Code:** 517019028

Federal College of Education (Technical), Yauri, Kebbi State was established by an Act of the National Assembly. The bill for the establishment was initiated and sponsored by Hon. Dr. Yusuf Tanko Sununu, who represented Yauri/Shanga/Ngaski Federal Constituency in the Federal House of Representatives. The bill was signed into law by President Muhammadu Buhari on July 20, 2022.

Kebbi State Government donated Government Day Secondary School, Tondi, Yauri, as the take-off site, along with 149,604 hectares for the permanent site. President Bola Tinubu appointed the pioneer principal officers in March 2025: Prof. Sa'adu Umar as Provost, Mr. Abdullahi Ahmed as Registrar, Dr. Muhammad Yusuf Alkali as Bursar, and Mrs. Maimuna Mohammed Ahmed as Librarian, with tenures of five years each as stipulated in the Federal Colleges of Education Act 2023.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college's mandate is to provide quality teacher education and entrepreneurial skills through cutting-edge technology and research-driven pedagogy; foster academic excellence; and prepare graduates to meet the evolving needs of the workforce while contributing meaningfully to national development. Core functions include: offering full-time courses in teaching, instruction, and training across disciplines including technology, applied science, arts, social sciences, humanities, and management; producing transformative leaders and innovators who drive sustainable development and technological advancement; training skilled educators and technicians for Nigeria's industrial and economic growth; and equipping students with practical, industry-relevant skills for the job market.

---

## 98. Federal College of Education, Gwoza
**MDA Code:** 517019029

Federal College of Education, Gwoza is located in Gwoza Local Government Area, Borno State, in the southeastern part of the state. The bill for establishing the college was sponsored by Senator Mohammed Ali Ndume and passed by the Senate in May 2021. The college is the first Federal College of Education in Borno State since the state was created in 1976.

Governor Babagana Zulum allocated Government Secondary School, Vile, Gwoza, as the temporary site in July 2023. President Bola Tinubu approved the immediate take-off in March 2024. On December 5, 2024, Governor Zulum handed over a completely remodeled edifice as the permanent site to the Federal Ministry of Education, along with a N200 million donation towards the college's take-off.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college was established with a visionary mandate to advance, strengthen, and democratise access to teacher education across Nigeria, addressing persistent barriers hindering Nigerians from obtaining quality professional training in education. Core functions include: providing and promoting sound basic education training as a foundation for national development, taking into account indigenous culture and enhancing national unity; providing higher education fostering systematic advancement of the science and art of teacher education; providing instruction in various branches of teacher education for research advancement and knowledge dissemination; offering NCE programmes in English, Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, TVET courses, and subjects creating wealth and accelerating development; and encouraging parents in insurgency-affected areas to send children to school through quality educational opportunities.

---

## 87. Federal College of Education (Technical), Keana
**MDA Code:** 517019030

Federal College of Education (Technical), Keana is a newly established federal institution located in Keana Local Government Area, Nasarawa State. The Act specifically establishing the college was assented to on February 8, 2023, and gazetted in Government Notice 7, No. 29 Vol. 110 of February 13, 2023. The college is also captured in the Federal Colleges of Education Act No. 43 (2023) published in the Federal Republic of Nigeria Official Gazette No. 132, Vol. 110, dated July 24, 2023.

The college's principal officers (Provost, Registrar, Bursar, and Librarian) were appointed in May 2023. The National Commission for Colleges of Education (NCCE) has granted approval to run 15 academic programmes across four schools housing 15 departments.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college's mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities, and management leading to the award of the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) and degrees. Core functions include: training qualified technical and vocational teachers for Nigeria's basic education system and technological development; producing educators with intellectual and professional background adequate for their teaching assignment; fostering adaptability to changing educational and national situations; preparing graduates for contributions to the world of work; conducting research in technical education; and organising workshops, seminars, and in-service courses to update practicing teachers' knowledge.

---

## 107. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF EDUCATION OFEME-OHUHU, ABIA STATE

**MDA Code:** 517019031

The Federal College of Education Ofeme-Ohuhu, Abia State, is one of six Federal Colleges of Education established in May 2020 by President Muhammadu Buhari's administration to address critical teacher shortages in Nigeria's education system. Located in Ofeme-Ohuhu, Abia State, representing additional allocation for the South-East geo-political zone, the college operates under the Federal Colleges of Education Act CAP F8 LFN 2004. The establishment followed National Commission for Colleges of Education's recognition that certain states lacked Federal Colleges of Education, representing policy intervention to address geographical imbalances in federal educational infrastructure and enhance teacher production capacity for Universal Basic Education implementation. The college's core mandate is to provide full-time courses of teaching, instruction, and training in technology, applied science, commerce, arts, social sciences, humanities and management. Specific statutory functions include: (a) to produce qualified teachers holding the Nigeria Certificate in Education (NCE) for primary and secondary schools; (b) to conduct research in development and adaptation of educational techniques and teaching methodologies; (c) to establish and maintain teaching units, schools, or extramural departments within or outside the college as Council may determine; (d) to establish further schools within the college by order of the Governing Council; (e) to institute and award fellowships, medals, prizes and other academic titles, distinctions, awards and forms of assistance; (f) to mount exhibitions and displays designed to foster appreciation of trends in educational scope and requirements; (g) to erect, provide, equip and maintain educational, recreational and residential facilities for students and staff; (h) to hold and manage any property whether movable or immovable; and (i) to perform such other acts and things whether or not incidental to the foregoing powers as may advance the objectives of the college as specified in the Act.

---

## 99. Federal College of Education, Ididep, Ibiono
**MDA Code:** 517019032

Federal College of Education, Ididep, Ibiono (FCE Ididep) is located in Ibiono Ibom Local Government Area of Akwa Ibom State. The college was established by the Federal Government as part of initiatives to expand access to quality teacher education in Nigeria. The site is the former Presbyterian Senior Science School, Ididep. Senator Ita Enang, former Senior Special Assistant on Niger Delta Affairs to the President, championed the establishment.

Governor Umo Eno of Akwa Ibom State granted a N95 million waiver for the Certificate of Occupancy in September 2023 to facilitate immediate take-off. The Akwa Ibom State Government officially handed over the Certificate of Occupancy to the Federal Government. The college is set on 94.927 hectares of land acquired by the Federal Government, and the affected Ididep community is receiving compensation.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mission is to become a global hub of academic excellence, focusing on innovative teaching, cutting-edge research, and training of motivated educators prepared to excel in various areas of specialisation. Core functions include: producing skilled and passionate teachers for foundational and basic education sectors; offering NCE programmes in Early Childhood Care and Education, English and Ibibio Languages, Christian Religious Studies, Social Studies, Mathematics, Computer Education, Business Education, and Agricultural Education; providing quality training with federal accreditation and quality assurance; creating opportunities for degree progression; and contributing to educational development in Akwa Ibom State and Nigeria at large. The college emphasises academic quality, discipline, and national development through professional teacher training.

---

## 100. Federal College of Education, Ilawe-Ekiti
**MDA Code:** 517019033

Federal College of Education, Ilawe-Ekiti, Ekiti State was established by the Federal Government of Nigeria in April 2023. In May 2023, President Muhammadu Buhari approved the establishment, and President Bola Tinubu approved the immediate take-off. The temporary site is the erstwhile Government Girls College, Ilawe-Ekiti, along Ilawe-Ikere Road, covering approximately 4 hectares.

The permanent site, also along Ilawe-Ikere Road, covers 107.813 hectares of land donated by the Ilawe community. The official handover of the temporary and permanent sites occurred on November 13, 2023. Dr. Gbade Ikuejube was appointed as the pioneer Provost. The Alawe of Ilawe-Ekiti, Oba Adebanji Ajibade Alabi, and the community celebrated the establishment as fulfillment of a great dream.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college mandate is to promote the production of qualified teachers at the foundational and basic segment of the education sector. It is designed as a two-in-one institution serving as both a College of Education and University, awarding both NCE Certificate and Degrees. Core functions include: running courses in Education, Sciences, and Technology; extending programmes to vocational and allied studies to produce job creators; equipping future educators with knowledge, skills, and professional values for effective teaching; offering NCE programmes across six schools: School of Arts and Social Sciences, School of Early Childhood Care and Primary Education, School of General Education, School of Languages, School of Science, and School of Vocational and Technical Education; and demonstrating the Federal Government's determination to expand educational opportunities and invest in training future educators.

---

## 290. FEDERAL UNIVERSITY OF TECHNOLOGY, AKURE

**MDA Code:** 517021017

Federal University of Technology Akure (FUTA), Ondo State, is a premier federal university of technology established in 1981 located in Akure, Ondo State capital. As leading technology university in Nigeria, FUTA provides undergraduate and postgraduate education in engineering, sciences, agriculture, environmental technology and management. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education and NUC regulation, FUTA is ranked among top Nigerian universities with strong emphasis on practical technology education. The university maintains collaborations with industries and international technology institutions conducting cutting-edge research.

**Core Mandate:** To provide world-class technology education, conduct innovative research and produce skilled engineers and technologists for Nigeria's industrial development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide undergraduate programmes in engineering, sciences, agriculture and environmental technology
(b) Offer postgraduate education including Master's and PhD in technological fields
(c) Conduct research on engineering, applied sciences and technology innovation
(d) Provide education in mechanical, electrical, civil, chemical, computer and agricultural engineering
(e) Offer programmes in industrial chemistry, physics, mathematics, computer science and earth sciences
(f) Conduct research on renewable energy, nanotechnology, materials science and biotechnology
(g) Award Bachelor of Technology (B.Tech), Master's and PhD degrees
(h) Maintain technology education standards through NUC and COREN accreditation
(i) Recruit highly qualified engineers, scientists and technologists
(j) Establish schools/faculties of engineering, sciences, agriculture and environmental technology
(k) Develop state-of-the-art engineering workshops, laboratories and research facilities
(l) Conduct industrial training (SIWES) ensuring students gain practical experience
(m) Promote innovation through research and development
(n) Establish strong university-industry partnerships for technology transfer
(o) Provide entrepreneurship development for technology-based businesses
(p) Develop technology incubation centers and innovation hubs
(q) Organize international technology conferences and engineering symposia
(r) Establish centers of excellence in engineering and technology
(s) Maintain full accreditation for all programmes
(t) Produce world-class engineers and technologists driving Nigeria's technological advancement

**MDA Code:** 517021066

Federal University of Health Sciences and Technology Tsafe, Zamfara State, is a newly established specialized federal university located in Tsafe, Zamfara State. As specialized health sciences institution, the university focuses on training health professionals, conducting health research and addressing healthcare challenges. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education and NUC regulation, the university addresses health workforce shortage in Zamfara State and North-West Nigeria while building capacity.

**Core Mandate:** To provide specialized health sciences education, conduct health research and train health professionals for Zamfara State and Nigeria.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide undergraduate programmes in medicine, nursing and allied health sciences
(b) Offer postgraduate education in health sciences
(c) Conduct health research addressing regional disease burden
(d) Provide education in medicine, dentistry, pharmacy and nursing
(e) Offer programmes in medical laboratory science, physiotherapy and radiography
(f) Conduct research on communicable diseases, maternal mortality and child health
(g) Award medical and health sciences degrees
(h) Maintain health education standards through NUC and professional councils
(i) Recruit qualified health educators and clinicians
(j) Establish faculties of medicine, nursing and health sciences
(k) Develop teaching hospital for clinical training
(l) Conduct community health outreach programmes
(m) Promote primary healthcare and disease prevention
(n) Establish partnerships with hospitals and health institutions
(o) Provide clinical training for health students
(p) Develop health ICT and telemedicine
(q) Organize health conferences and medical research symposia
(r) Establish health research centers
(s) Seek accreditation for health programmes
(t) Address health workforce shortage and improve healthcare delivery in Zamfara State
