## 66. DIVISION OF AGRICULTURAL COLLEGE, ABU ZARIA
**MDA Code:** 517022001

The Division of Agricultural College at Ahmadu Bello University (ABU), Zaria, operates as a specialized unit focused on agricultural education, training, and extension services. Ahmadu Bello University, established in 1962 and located in Zaria, Kaduna State, is one of Nigeria's premier universities with a strong tradition in agricultural sciences. The Division works to advance agricultural knowledge and practice through education, research, and community engagement.

The Division's mandate includes providing agricultural education at various levels including diploma, certificate, and short courses; conducting research in agricultural sciences relevant to Nigeria's food security and agricultural development; providing extension services to farming communities in the surrounding regions; training agricultural technicians and middle-level manpower for the agricultural sector; developing and disseminating improved agricultural practices and technologies; supporting farmer education and empowerment programmes; and collaborating with agricultural research institutes, extension agencies, and farmer organizations.

The Division contributes to ABU's broader mission in agricultural development, which includes the Faculty of Agriculture and the Institute for Agricultural Research. It plays a role in addressing Nigeria's need for trained agricultural professionals who can support farmers, agribusinesses, and agricultural development programmes. The Division's programmes are designed to produce graduates with practical skills applicable to various aspects of agricultural production, processing, and marketing, contributing to Nigeria's agricultural transformation agenda.

---

## 287. FEDERAL UNIVERSITY OF PETROLEUM RESOURCES, EFFURUN

**MDA Code:** 517023001

Federal University of Petroleum Resources Effurun (FUPRE), Delta State, is Africa's first specialized petroleum university established March 27, 2007 by Presidential directive and signed into law October 2017. Located in Effurun (permanent site at Ugbomro), Delta State in Niger Delta, FUPRE is sixth petroleum university globally. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education and NUC regulation, the university has tripodal mandate of teaching, research and extension services for petroleum and allied sectors. FUPRE offers programmes in petroleum engineering, chemical engineering, geology, geophysics, environmental management and petroleum business. The university maintains international partnerships and collaborates with oil companies.

**Core Mandate:** To provide world-class petroleum education, conduct cutting-edge oil and gas research and deliver extension services for petroleum industry in Nigeria and globally.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide undergraduate programmes in petroleum engineering, chemical engineering, geology and geophysics
(b) Offer postgraduate education including Master's and PhD in petroleum-related fields
(c) Conduct research on oil and gas exploration, production, refining and distribution
(d) Provide education in petroleum geology, reservoir engineering and drilling engineering
(e) Offer programmes in petroleum chemistry, gas engineering and petrochemicals
(f) Conduct research on environmental management in oil and gas operations
(g) Award degrees in petroleum engineering and allied petroleum sciences
(h) Maintain international petroleum education standards through NUC accreditation
(i) Recruit qualified petroleum engineers and oil industry experts
(j) Establish faculties/colleges of petroleum studies, engineering and environmental sciences
(k) Develop petroleum laboratories, pilot plants and research facilities
(l) Conduct consultancy services for oil companies and petroleum industry
(m) Promote local content development in Nigeria's petroleum sector
(n) Establish partnerships with international petroleum universities and oil companies
(o) Provide industrial training and internships in oil and gas companies
(p) Develop petroleum technology and innovation centers
(q) Organize petroleum conferences and oil industry workshops
(r) Establish petroleum research institutes and centers of excellence
(s) Collaborate with Royal Academy of Engineering and international bodies
(t) Produce high-level petroleum manpower reducing expatriate dependence in Nigeria's oil industry

## 73. FEDERAL SCHOOL OF COOPERATIVES (F.S.C.) SOKOTO
**MDA Code:** 517026001

The Federal School of Cooperatives, Sokoto, is an educational institution established to provide training in cooperative studies and related disciplines, contributing to manpower development for Nigeria's cooperative sector. Cooperatives have historically played important roles in Nigeria's agricultural and economic development, particularly in crop marketing, input supply, credit provision, and rural development. The school is part of the network of federal cooperative colleges supervised by the Federal Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Development.

The school's mandate includes providing formal cooperative education through certificate and diploma programmes; training cooperative society officers, managers, and board members; conducting short courses and workshops on cooperative management and governance; developing curricula relevant to modern cooperative practice; conducting research on cooperative development issues; providing consultancy services to cooperative societies and government agencies; promoting cooperative principles and best practices; and supporting the government's cooperative reform and revival initiatives.

The school's programmes cover cooperative economics and management, cooperative law, accounting for cooperatives, cooperative governance, and related business disciplines. Graduates are equipped to serve in cooperative societies, government cooperative departments, agricultural organizations, and financial institutions. The school contributes to the Federal Government's agenda of reviving and reforming cooperative societies as vehicles for economic empowerment, particularly in agriculture. The Sokoto location enables the school to serve the cooperative development needs of the northern region while contributing to national cooperative education objectives.

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 301-320 (FEDERAL GOVERNMENT COLLEGES)

## 301. FGC AZARE

**MDA Code:** 517026002

Federal Government College Azare, Bauchi State, is a federal unity school located in Azare, Bauchi State in North-East Nigeria. Established as part of Federal Government Colleges (Unity Schools) system created to promote national integration, the school provides secondary education bringing together students from diverse ethnic, religious and geographical backgrounds. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education, FGC Azare admits students through National Common Entrance Examination using 60% merit and 40% quota system ensuring national spread. The school offers WAEC and NECO examinations preparing students for tertiary education.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality secondary education promoting national unity, integration and academic excellence for Nigerian youth from diverse backgrounds.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide secondary education from JSS1 to SS3 for students aged 10-18 years
(b) Admit students through National Common Entrance Examination ensuring national spread
(c) Implement 60% merit-based and 40% quota-based admission system
(d) Promote national unity by bringing together students from all states and ethnic groups
(e) Provide boarding facilities accommodating students from across Nigeria
(f) Deliver Nigerian national curriculum prescribed by Federal Ministry of Education
(g) Prepare students for WAEC (WASSCE) and NECO (SSCE) examinations
(h) Provide co-curricular activities including sports, cultural activities and clubs
(i) Maintain high academic standards as Unity School
(j) Employ qualified teachers meeting federal service requirements
(k) Provide science laboratories, libraries and educational infrastructure
(l) Foster inter-ethnic understanding, tolerance and national cohesion among students
(m) Develop students' leadership skills through prefect systems and student government
(n) Provide moral, civic and character education
(o) Maintain discipline and conducive learning environment
(p) Provide healthcare services through school clinic
(q) Implement school feeding programme for boarding students
(r) Organize inter-house sports and academic competitions
(s) Prepare students for university entrance examinations (JAMB/UTME)
(t) Produce well-rounded graduates contributing to national development and unity

---

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 321-350 (FEDERAL GOVERNMENT COLLEGES)

## 321. FGC MINNA

**MDA Code:** 517026022

Federal Government College Minna, Niger State, is a federal unity school located in Minna, Niger State capital in North-Central Nigeria. Established as Unity School, the school provides secondary education promoting national integration. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education, FGC Minna admits students from across Nigeria through National Common Entrance Examination bringing together diverse ethnic and religious backgrounds.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality secondary education promoting national unity and academic excellence in Niger State.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide secondary education from JSS1 to SS3 for students aged 10-18 years
(b) Admit students through National Common Entrance Examination ensuring national spread
(c) Implement 60% merit-based and 40% quota-based admission system
(d) Promote national unity by bringing together students from all states and ethnic groups
(e) Provide boarding facilities accommodating students from across Nigeria
(f) Deliver Nigerian national curriculum prescribed by Federal Ministry of Education
(g) Prepare students for WAEC (WASSCE) and NECO (SSCE) examinations
(h) Provide co-curricular activities including sports, cultural activities and clubs
(i) Maintain high academic standards as Unity School
(j) Employ qualified teachers meeting federal service requirements
(k) Provide science laboratories, libraries and educational infrastructure
(l) Foster inter-ethnic understanding, tolerance and national cohesion among students
(m) Develop students' leadership skills through prefect systems and student government
(n) Provide moral, civic and character education
(o) Maintain discipline and conducive learning environment
(p) Provide healthcare services through school clinic
(q) Implement school feeding programme for boarding students
(r) Organize inter-house sports and academic competitions
(s) Prepare students for university entrance examinations (JAMB/UTME)
(t) Produce well-rounded graduates contributing to national development and unity

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 351-380

## 351. FGGC EFON ALAYE

**MDA Code:** 517026052

Federal Government Girls College Efon Alaye, Ekiti State, is a federal girls-only unity school located in Efon Alaye, Ekiti State in South-West Nigeria. Established as part of Federal Government Girls Colleges system to promote national unity and girls' education, the school provides secondary education exclusively for female students from JSS1 to SS3. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education, FGGC Efon Alaye admits girls from across Nigeria through National Common Entrance Examination using 60% merit and 40% quota system. The school provides boarding facilities bringing together girls from diverse ethnic, religious and socio-economic backgrounds. FGGC Efon Alaye promotes women empowerment and national integration in Ekiti State context.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality secondary education for girls promoting national unity, women empowerment and academic excellence in Ekiti State.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide secondary education exclusively for female students from JSS1 to SS3 aged 10-18 years
(b) Admit girls nationally through Common Entrance Examination ensuring geographical spread across all states
(c) Implement 60% merit-based and 40% quota-based admission system for female students
(d) Promote national unity by bringing together girls from all states, ethnic groups and religious backgrounds
(e) Provide girls-only boarding facilities with appropriate security, safety measures and gender-sensitive infrastructure
(f) Deliver Nigerian national curriculum prescribed by Federal Ministry of Education with gender-sensitive pedagogical approach
(g) Prepare girls for WAEC (WASSCE) and NECO (SSCE) examinations
(h) Provide co-curricular activities promoting girls' holistic development including sports, arts, drama, debates and clubs
(i) Maintain high academic standards befitting Federal Unity School status
(j) Employ qualified teachers including female role models, mentors and counselors
(k) Provide educational infrastructure including science laboratories, libraries, ICT facilities and workshops suitable for girls
(l) Foster inter-ethnic, inter-religious and inter-regional understanding among female students
(m) Develop girls' leadership skills, confidence, self-esteem, assertiveness and public speaking abilities
(n) Provide character, moral, civic and values education addressing girls' development needs
(o) Ensure safe, secure learning environment addressing girls' specific safety, health and privacy needs
(p) Provide healthcare services including reproductive health education, menstrual hygiene management and counseling
(q) Implement school feeding programme for boarding students ensuring nutrition
(r) Organize inter-house sports competitions, academic competitions and talent development programmes
(s) Prepare girls for university entrance examinations (JAMB/UTME) and career development
(t) Empower girls as future leaders for national development, unity and women advancement in Nigeria

---

## 352. FGGC GOMBE

**MDA Code:** 517026053

Federal Government Girls College Gombe, Gombe State, is a federal girls-only unity school located in Gombe, Gombe State capital in North-East Nigeria. Established as part of Unity Schools system to promote national unity and girls' education, the school provides secondary education exclusively for female students. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education, FGGC Gombe admits girls from across Nigeria through National Common Entrance Examination using merit-quota admission system. The school provides boarding education bringing together girls from diverse backgrounds promoting women empowerment in North-East region facing educational challenges.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality secondary education for girls promoting national unity and women empowerment in Gombe State and North-East Nigeria.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide secondary education exclusively for female students JSS1-SS3
(b) Admit girls nationally through Common Entrance Examination ensuring national representation
(c) Implement 60% merit and 40% quota admission system for girls
(d) Promote national unity among diverse female student population from all regions
(e) Provide girls-only boarding facilities with enhanced security and appropriate amenities
(f) Deliver federal curriculum with gender sensitivity and women empowerment focus
(g) Prepare girls for WAEC and NECO examinations
(h) Provide co-curricular activities including sports, cultural programmes and leadership training
(i) Maintain high academic standards despite regional challenges
(j) Employ qualified teachers including female educators as role models
(k) Provide educational infrastructure including laboratories and libraries
(l) Foster inter-ethnic understanding among female students from Nigeria's diverse communities
(m) Develop girls' leadership, confidence and decision-making skills
(n) Provide character, moral and civic education for girls
(o) Ensure safe environment addressing girls' specific needs in North-East context
(p) Provide healthcare including reproductive health education and counseling services
(q) Implement school feeding programme ensuring student nutrition
(r) Organize competitions, talent shows and skills development programmes
(s) Prepare girls for university admission and career paths
(t) Empower girls for national development and women advancement addressing gender gaps in North-East

## 353. FGGC ENUGU

Established to promote national unity and girls' education, the school provides secondary education exclusively for female students. Operating under Federal Ministry of Education, FGGC Enugu admits girls from across Nigeria's 36 states through National Common Entrance Examination. The school brings together girls from diverse ethnic, religious and geographical backgrounds in historic South-East Nigeria city promoting national integration and women empowerment.

(c) Implement merit-quota admission system balancing excellence and national representation

(e) Provide secure boarding facilities with gender-appropriate infrastructure and safety measures
(f) Deliver national curriculum with gender-sensitive pedagogical approaches
(g) Prepare girls for national examinations (WAEC WASSCE and NECO SSCE)
(h) Provide co-curricular activities promoting girls' holistic growth including sports, arts and debates
(i) Maintain academic excellence standards befitting Unity School
(j) Employ qualified female and male teachers providing role models
(k) Provide educational facilities including science laboratories, libraries and ICT centers
(l) Foster ethnic and religious tolerance through integrated living and learning
(m) Develop leadership skills, self-confidence and assertiveness in female students
(n) Provide moral, character and civic education addressing girls' developmental needs
(o) Ensure safe, secure learning environment protecting girls' wellbeing
(p) Provide comprehensive healthcare including reproductive health education
(q) Implement feeding programme ensuring boarding students' nutrition
(r) Organize competitive academic, sports and cultural events
(s) Prepare girls for tertiary education entry through JAMB/UTME coaching
(t) Empower girls as future leaders and nation-builders promoting gender equality

## 168. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF HEALTH AND SOCIAL WELFARE - HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 521001001

The Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare was created through merger in October 2024 cabinet reshuffle, combining Federal Ministry of Health with social welfare and humanitarian functions. The Ministry is responsible for national health policies, healthcare delivery systems, disease control, pharmaceutical regulation, social welfare programmes and humanitarian services. Led by Minister of Health and Social Welfare assisted by Ministers of State and Permanent Secretary. The Ministry coordinates Nigeria's health sector encompassing primary healthcare, secondary healthcare, tertiary healthcare, public health, reproductive health and nutrition. Key agencies include National Primary Health Care Development Agency (NPHCDA), Nigeria Centre for Disease Control and Prevention (NCDC), National Agency for Food and Drug Administration and Control (NAFDAC), National Health Insurance Authority (NHIA), Teaching Hospitals, Federal Medical Centres, Federal Psychiatric Hospitals, and social welfare agencies.

**Core Mandate:** To ensure health for all Nigerians through formulation and implementation of health and social welfare policies, coordinating healthcare delivery and protecting vulnerable populations.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate and implement national health policies including National Health Policy
(b) Coordinate primary healthcare development through NPHCDA
(c) Oversee federal tertiary hospitals including Teaching Hospitals, Federal Medical Centres
(d) Regulate pharmaceuticals, food and medical products through NAFDAC
(e) Coordinate disease surveillance and epidemic response through NCDC
(f) Manage public health programmes including immunization, malaria control, TB control, HIV/AIDS
(g) Implement National Health Insurance Scheme promoting universal health coverage
(h) Coordinate reproductive, maternal, newborn, child and adolescent health programmes
(i) Manage nutrition interventions and food security programmes
(j) Regulate medical and health professions through professional councils
(k) Coordinate health workforce development and training
(l) Manage health infrastructure development in federal health facilities
(m) Coordinate emergency medical services and disaster health response
(n) Implement International Health Regulations compliance and global health security
(o) Coordinate social welfare programmes for vulnerable groups
(p) Manage social protection schemes and safety net programmes
(q) Coordinate humanitarian response for internally displaced persons and refugees
(r) Implement child protection services and prevent violence against children
(s) Coordinate poverty alleviation interventions
(t) Manage health information systems and data management

---

## 62. DENTAL TECHNOLOGISTS REGISTRATION BOARD OF NIGERIA
**MDA Code:** 521007001

The Dental Technologists Registration Board of Nigeria was established by the Dental Technologists (Registration, etc.) Act (now CAP D6 LFN 2004) to regulate the profession of dental technology in Nigeria. Dental technology is defined in the Act as the art and science of established laboratory procedures associated with the construction of dental prostheses and restoration according to prescription. The Board operates under the Federal Ministry of Health and is charged with comprehensive regulatory responsibilities for the profession.

The Board's statutory functions include determining what standards of knowledge and skill are to be attained by persons seeking to become members of the profession and improving those standards from time to time as circumstances may permit; securing, in accordance with the Act, the establishment and maintenance of a register of persons registered as members of the profession and the publication of lists of those persons; conducting examinations in the profession and awarding certificates or diplomas to successful candidates; prescribing fees for examinations, registration, and other services; and performing other functions conferred by the Act.

The Board maintains the register of qualified dental technologists and issues registration certificates. It establishes and reviews codes of conduct for professional practice and has established a Dental Technologists Disciplinary Committee to consider cases of professional misconduct referred by the Investigating Panel. The Board approves training institutions and their curricula, conducts accreditation visits, and determines which qualifications (domestic and foreign) are acceptable for registration purposes. The minimum qualifications accepted by the Board are specified in the Third Schedule to the Act, ensuring that only properly trained practitioners are authorized to practice dental technology in Nigeria.

---

## 86. Federal College of Dental Technology and Therapy, Enugu
**MDA Code:** 521016001

The Federal College of Dental Technology and Therapy, Enugu is a federal tertiary institution located in Trans-Ekulu, Enugu State. It was established in 1955 in Lagos as the Federal School of Dental Technology and Hygiene, training dental technicians and hygienists. In 1978, it was renamed the Federal School of Dental Technology and Therapy with the expanded goal of training dental technologists and therapists. The school relocated to its present location in Enugu in 1982.

The institution was re-established by Nigerian law via the Federal College of Dental Technology and Therapy (Establishment) Act No. 8 of 2017, which granted the power to award degrees. President Muhammadu Buhari signed the Act on December 5, 2017. In February 2021, the National Universities Commission approved the college to run undergraduate degree programmes in collaboration with Federal University of Technology, Owerri.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
The college is mandated to provide academic and professional training in dental technology and therapy to meet Nigeria's critical healthcare manpower needs. Under Section 6(a) of the Establishment Act, the college is empowered to award degrees, diplomas, and certificates for courses provided under the Act. Core functions include: producing dental technologists and dental therapists for various medical institutions; providing patient care and continuing education opportunities; contributing research discoveries to improve dental healthcare; offering National Diploma, Higher National Diploma, and Bachelor of Technology degree programmes in dental technology and therapy; and serving as a recognised Centre of Excellence in Africa for dental technology and therapy education. The school was once the only institution training dental technologists and therapists in Nigeria.

---

## 69. ENVIRONMENTAL HEALTH OFFICERS TUTORS, IBADAN
**MDA Code:** 521017001

The Environmental Health Officers Tutors Training Centre in Ibadan is a specialized institution dedicated to training instructors and educators in the field of environmental health. The centre operates under the Federal Ministry of Health/Federal Ministry of Environment and works to develop qualified tutors who can deliver quality training to environmental health professionals across Nigeria's schools of health technology and related institutions.

The centre's mandate includes providing pedagogical training to environmental health professionals preparing to become tutors and instructors; developing curriculum and teaching methodologies for environmental health education; training tutors in modern instructional techniques and educational technologies; upgrading the skills of existing environmental health tutors; conducting research in environmental health education and training methods; developing instructional materials and resources for environmental health programmes; and supporting the standardization of environmental health education across Nigeria.

The establishment of a dedicated tutors training centre reflects the importance of quality instruction in producing competent environmental health officers. Environmental health professionals play crucial roles in disease prevention, sanitation enforcement, food safety inspection, and community health promotion. By training tutors who can effectively transfer knowledge and skills, the centre contributes to the development of a competent environmental health workforce capable of addressing Nigeria's public health challenges. The centre works in coordination with the Environmental Health Officers Registration Council of Nigeria (EHORECON) to ensure training programmes meet professional standards.

## 248. FEDERAL TEACHING HOSPITAL, ABAKALIKI

**MDA Code:** 521026017

Federal Teaching Hospital Abakaliki (FETHA), Ebonyi State, is a federal tertiary healthcare institution serving as teaching hospital for medical programmes. Located in Abakaliki, Ebonyi State capital, the hospital provides tertiary specialized medical care while training medical students, doctors, nurses and other health professionals. Ebonyi State, created in 1996, relies significantly on FETHA for advanced healthcare. The hospital serves as referral center for Ebonyi and surrounding states. Operating under Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare, FETHA combines clinical services, medical education and research.

**Core Mandate:** To provide tertiary specialized healthcare, train healthcare professionals and conduct medical research serving Ebonyi State and South-East Nigeria.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide tertiary specialized medical services across all major clinical disciplines
(b) Serve as teaching hospital for medical school training doctors
(c) Provide clinical training for nursing students and other health professionals
(d) Operate 24-hour emergency department for acute cases
(e) Provide specialist consultations in internal medicine, surgery, pediatrics, obstetrics and gynecology
(f) Offer subspecialty services including cardiology, nephrology, neurology and oncology
(g) Conduct complex surgical procedures including neurosurgery, cardiac surgery and orthopedics
(h) Provide comprehensive maternal and child health services
(i) Operate intensive care units (ICU) and high-dependency units
(j) Conduct diagnostic services including radiology, laboratory and imaging
(k) Provide cancer treatment including chemotherapy and radiotherapy
(l) Offer dialysis services for kidney disease patients
(m) Conduct medical research advancing healthcare knowledge
(n) Provide residency training for specialist doctor certification
(o) Offer continuing medical education for healthcare professionals
(p) Serve as referral center for secondary hospitals in region
(q) Provide public health services and disease surveillance
(r) Conduct outreach medical programmes to underserved communities
(s) Maintain teaching hospital standards for accreditation
(t) Contribute to health policy development through research and expertise

## 51. DAVID UMAHI FEDERAL UNIVERSITY TEACHING HOSPITAL, UBURU, EBONYI STATE
**MDA Code:** 521026022

David Umahi Federal University Teaching Hospital (DUFUTH), Uburu, is a federal tertiary health institution located in Ohaozara Local Government Area of Ebonyi State. The hospital was established as the teaching hospital component of the David Umahi Federal University of Health Sciences (DUFUHS). Originally established as part of the King David Nweze Umahi University of Medical Sciences by the Ebonyi State Government, the institution was taken over by the Federal Government on September 6, 2022, following an agreement signed between the Federal Ministry of Health and the Ebonyi State Government.

DUFUTH operates as a premier healthcare institution with ultra-modern facilities, cutting-edge equipment, and innovative medical technology. The hospital's vision is to be a world-class tertiary health institution committed to effective and efficient health care delivery, qualitative training, and research. Its mission centers on an irreversible commitment to delivering world-class care with ultra-modern medical facilities, a conducive environment, and passion-driven personnel.

The hospital is mandated to provide clinical training for medical and health sciences students of DUFUHS and other affiliated institutions while rendering specialized healthcare services to patients. DUFUTH has established multiple centres of excellence including oncology, kidney transplant with dialysis centre, liver transplant facilities, and comprehensive cancer management services. The institution operates with state-of-the-art diagnostic equipment and aims to halt medical tourism abroad by providing world-class treatment within Nigeria. The hospital has activated primary healthcare centres across Ebonyi State communities including Umuka, Ugwulangwu, Ngbo, and Nwofe to extend its services to rural populations. Under the leadership of the Chief Medical Director, DUFUTH conducts free medical outreach programmes and has performed hundreds of successful surgeries in various specialized areas.

## 281. FEDERAL UNIVERSITY OF HEALTH SCIENCES TEACHING HOSPITAL, OTUKPO, BENUE STATE

Operating under Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare, the hospital combines clinical services, medical education and health research serving Benue State and surrounding areas in North-Central Nigeria.

(q) Conduct public health programmes and disease surveillance

(s) Maintain teaching hospital accreditation standards for medical education
(t) Collaborate with Federal University of Health Sciences on medical curriculum development and research

## 132. FEDERAL MEDICAL CENTRE - ABUJA

**MDA Code:** 521027003

The Federal Medical Centre, Abuja, is a federal tertiary healthcare institution providing specialized medical services, training of healthcare professionals and conducting medical research. Located in Nigeria's Federal Capital Territory, the institution operates under Federal Ministry of Health as one of Nigeria's network of Federal Medical Centres established to provide tertiary healthcare services, complement teaching hospitals and ensure equitable distribution of specialized medical facilities. While not a full teaching hospital affiliated to a university, Federal Medical Centres perform similar functions providing specialist services, clinical training for medical students and residency training for postgraduate medical education. The Centre's core mandate is to provide comprehensive tertiary healthcare services, train healthcare professionals and conduct medical research. Specific functions include: (a) to provide specialized medical services across major clinical specialties including internal medicine, surgery, pediatrics, obstetrics and gynecology, orthopedics, ophthalmology, ear-nose-throat, psychiatry and other specialties; (b) to offer emergency and trauma care services on 24-hour basis; (c) to provide diagnostic services including laboratory investigations, medical imaging (X-ray, ultrasound, CT scan, MRI), endoscopy and specialized diagnostic procedures; (d) to operate intensive care units (ICU), high dependency units (HDU) and specialized care units; (e) to provide inpatient services with adequate bed capacity across medical, surgical, pediatric, obstetric and other wards; (f) to offer outpatient clinic services across various specialties; (g) to provide maternal and child health services including antenatal care, delivery services, postnatal care and pediatric services; (h) to operate theatre facilities for surgical procedures across specialties; (i) to provide clinical training for medical students from affiliated universities through clinical rotations and attachments; (j) to conduct residency training programmes for postgraduate medical education in various specialties; (k) to provide internship training for medical graduates, pharmacists, laboratory scientists and other health professionals; (l) to offer continuing professional development programmes for healthcare workers; (m) to conduct clinical and health systems research; (n) to provide public health programmes including health education, disease prevention and health promotion; (o) to serve as referral center receiving cases from secondary healthcare facilities; (p) to collaborate with teaching hospitals, specialist hospitals and research institutes; (q) to maintain pharmacy services providing pharmaceutical care; (r) to provide physiotherapy, occupational therapy and rehabilitation services; and (s) to establish satellite clinics and outreach services for underserved populations.

## 190. FEDERAL NEURO-PSYCHIATRIC HOSPITAL YABA

**MDA Code:** 521027009

The Federal Neuro-Psychiatric Hospital, Yaba, Lagos, is Nigeria's oldest and largest psychiatric hospital, originally established in 1907 as Yaba Asylum during colonial era. Located in Yaba, Lagos, the hospital serves as premier mental health institution in Nigeria and West Africa. Renamed Federal Neuro-Psychiatric Hospital following Nigeria's independence to reduce stigma associated with "asylum" terminology, the hospital provides comprehensive neuropsychiatric services, conducts mental health research, trains mental health professionals and serves as referral center for Lagos and surrounding states. The hospital has historical significance as pioneering institution for mental healthcare in Nigeria.

**Core Mandate:** To provide comprehensive tertiary neuropsychiatric care, conduct mental health research, train mental health professionals and serve as national reference center for mental health.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Provide neurological services including epilepsy management and neurological rehabilitation

(f) Provide emergency mental health services on 24-hour basis

(h) Provide substance abuse treatment and addiction recovery programmes
(i) Offer child and adolescent psychiatric services
(j) Provide geriatric mental health services
(k) Conduct psychological assessments and psychometric testing
(l) Provide individual, group and family psychotherapy
(m) Offer electroconvulsive therapy (ECT) for treatment-resistant conditions
(n) Conduct mental health research advancing psychiatric knowledge
(o) Train psychiatrists, clinical psychologists, psychiatric nurses and psychiatric social workers
(p) Provide continuing medical education for mental health professionals
(q) Conduct community mental health outreach and health education
(r) Operate psychiatric rehabilitation and occupational therapy programmes
(s) Maintain psychiatric pharmaceutical services and medication management
(t) Serve as resource center for mental health policy development

## 85. Federal College of Complementary and Alternative Medicine, Nigeria
**MDA Code:** 521027011

The Federal College of Complementary and Alternative Medicine (FEDCAM) was established by the Federal Government of Nigeria as a parastatal of the Federal Ministry of Health based on recommendations from the Medical and Dental Council of Nigeria (MDCN). The Federal College of Complementary and Alternative Medicine (Establishment) Act 2021 was passed by the National Assembly as an unconventional institution for the pursuit of academic and professional knowledge in complementary and alternative medicine.

The institution's academic activities were suspended by the National Universities Commission (NUC) in October 2010 for running degree programmes without mandatory approvals. In 2011, the Federal Ministry of Health suspended the management and instituted an Interim Administrative Team. There are ongoing plans to revive FEDCAM as part of the Federal Government's initiative to integrate traditional medicine into the national healthcare system.

**Core Mandate and Functions:**
FEDCAM was established with the mandate to train alternative medicine practitioners in healthcare delivery services; carry out clinical works; provide alternative methods of treating ailments; and conduct research in complementary and alternative medicine. Its functions include training and retraining practitioners of Complementary and Alternative Medicine in Nigeria; researching and developing all aspects of Complementary and Alternative Medicine; contributing to formulating regulatory standards; and supporting integration of complementary and alternative medicine into the mainstream Nigerian healthcare delivery system. The college covers modalities including Homeopathy, Naturopathy, Acupuncture, Osteopathy, and Chiropractic practices.

## 158. FEDERAL MEDICAL CENTRE, UMUAHIA

**MDA Code:** 521027017

The Federal Medical Centre, Umuahia, is a federal tertiary healthcare institution located in Umuahia, Abia State capital, South-East Nigeria. Established under Federal Ministry of Health, the Centre provides specialized healthcare services to Abia State and surrounding areas in South-East region. Umuahia's position as Abia State capital creates significant healthcare demands requiring tertiary facility. ## 133. FEDERAL MEDICAL CENTRE ABEOKUTA

**MDA Code:** 521027019

The Federal Medical Centre, Abeokuta, is a federal tertiary healthcare institution located in Abeokuta, Ogun State, Nigeria. Established under Federal Ministry of Health, the Centre is one of Federal Medical Centres network providing specialized healthcare services in Nigeria. Federal Medical Centres were established to expand access to tertiary healthcare services beyond teaching hospitals, ensure equitable distribution of specialist medical facilities across geopolitical zones, serve as referral centers for secondary healthcare facilities, and provide clinical training platforms for medical education. Located in Abeokuta, Ogun State capital, the Centre serves Ogun State and surrounding areas providing comprehensive tertiary medical services. The Centre's core mandate is to provide comprehensive specialized medical care, train healthcare professionals and conduct medical research. Specific functions include: (a) to provide tertiary-level healthcare services across major clinical specialties including internal medicine, general surgery, pediatrics, obstetrics and gynecology, orthopedics, ophthalmology, ENT, psychiatry and other disciplines; (b) to operate as referral center receiving complex cases from general hospitals and primary healthcare centers; (c) to provide 24-hour emergency and accident services with adequately equipped emergency department; (d) to offer comprehensive diagnostic services including clinical pathology, medical microbiology, chemical pathology, hematology, medical imaging (radiology, ultrasonography, CT scan) and specialized investigations; (e) to operate intensive care unit (ICU), high dependency unit (HDU) and special care baby unit (SCBU); (f) to provide inpatient services across medical, surgical, pediatric, obstetric and gynecological wards; (g) to offer specialist outpatient clinic services in various medical specialties; (h) to provide maternal and neonatal healthcare services including antenatal care, safe delivery services, postnatal care and family planning; (i) to operate theater complex for surgical procedures across specialties; (j) to provide clinical training for undergraduate medical students through affiliation with medical schools; (k) to conduct postgraduate medical training including residency programmes in various specialties; (l) to offer internship training for medical doctors, pharmacists, medical laboratory scientists, nurses and other health professionals; (m) to provide continuing medical education and professional development for healthcare workers; (n) to conduct clinical research and participate in multi-center research trials; (o) to provide pharmacy services with adequate drug supply and pharmaceutical care; (p) to offer physiotherapy, occupational therapy and rehabilitation services; (q) to establish disease surveillance and infection control systems; (r) to provide public health services including health education and community outreach; and (s) to maintain quality assurance systems ensuring patient safety and clinical governance.

The Federal School of Medical Laboratory Jos, Plateau State, is a federal health training institution providing specialized training in medical laboratory science. The School trains Medical Laboratory Scientists at diploma level to work in hospital laboratories, diagnostic centers, public health laboratories and research institutions. Located in Jos, Plateau State, the institution operates under Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare. Graduates obtain certification enabling registration with Medical Laboratory Science Council of Nigeria (MLSCN) as qualified Medical Laboratory Scientists.

**Core Mandate:** To train competent Medical Laboratory Scientists for diagnostic services, public health laboratories and medical research.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide National Diploma in Medical Laboratory Science
(b) Train students in clinical chemistry and biochemistry laboratory techniques
(c) Provide training in hematology and blood banking
(d) Offer training in medical microbiology and parasitology
(e) Conduct training in histopathology and cytology
(f) Provide training in immunology and serology
(g) Offer training in molecular diagnostics and genetics
(h) Conduct training in laboratory quality assurance and safety
(i) Provide training in medical laboratory instrumentation and equipment
(j) Offer training in phlebotomy and specimen collection
(k) Conduct clinical rotations in teaching hospitals and laboratories
(l) Provide training in laboratory information management systems
(m) Offer training in public health laboratory services
(n) Conduct training in research methodology and data analysis
(o) Provide training in laboratory management and administration
(p) Prepare students for Medical Laboratory Science Council registration
(q) Conduct continuous assessment and final examinations
(r) Maintain laboratory training facilities with modern equipment
(s) Collaborate with teaching hospitals for practical training
(t) Produce Medical Laboratory Scientists for healthcare system

## 191. FEDERAL NEURO-PSYCHIATRIC HOSPITAL, BUDO-EGBA, KWARA STATE

**MDA Code:** 521027048

The Federal Neuro-Psychiatric Hospital, Budo-Egba, Kwara State (near Ilorin), is a federal tertiary mental health facility serving North-Central Nigeria. Established to expand access to specialized mental health services in Middle Belt region, the hospital addresses mental health needs of Kwara State and neighboring states including Kogi, Niger and parts of Oyo State. Located in Budo-Egba community along Ilorin-Jebba road, the hospital provides comprehensive neuropsychiatric services following international best practices in mental healthcare while respecting local cultural contexts.

**Core Mandate:** To provide comprehensive tertiary neuropsychiatric services, promote mental health awareness and train mental health professionals for North-Central region.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide specialized psychiatric treatment for mental and behavioral disorders
(b) Provide neurological services for epilepsy and other neurological conditions
(c) Operate inpatient psychiatric facilities with acute and chronic care wards
(d) Provide outpatient psychiatric clinics and community mental health services
(e) Offer emergency psychiatric services and crisis intervention
(f) Conduct psychiatric assessments and diagnostic evaluations
(g) Provide addiction treatment services and substance abuse counseling
(h) Offer child and adolescent mental health services
(i) Provide mental health services for elderly patients
(j) Conduct psychological therapy including cognitive behavioral therapy
(k) Provide psychosocial rehabilitation and vocational rehabilitation
(l) Offer occupational therapy and recreational therapy
(m) Train psychiatrists, nurses and mental health technicians
(n) Conduct mental health research relevant to North-Central region
(o) Provide mental health education and anti-stigma campaigns
(p) Offer family therapy and caregiver support services
(q) Conduct mental health screening in communities
(r) Provide consultation services to general hospitals in region
(s) Maintain psychiatric medications supply and pharmacy services
(t) Collaborate with traditional healers on culturally appropriate mental health care

**MDA Code:** 521027058

The Federal Medical Centre, Onitsha, is a federal tertiary healthcare institution located in Onitsha, Anambra State, Nigeria. Established under Federal Ministry of Health, the Centre is part of Federal Medical Centres network providing specialized healthcare services nationwide. Located in Onitsha, Nigeria's major commercial city and trading hub in South-East Nigeria, the Centre serves Onitsha, Anambra State and surrounding areas in South-East and South-South regions. Onitsha's strategic location as commercial center with large population and industrial activities creates significant healthcare demands requiring specialized medical facilities. The Centre's core mandate is to provide comprehensive tertiary healthcare services, facilitate training of healthcare professionals and conduct medical research. (d) to provide comprehensive diagnostic services including clinical laboratory (hematology, chemical pathology, medical microbiology, histopathology), medical imaging (radiology, ultrasonography, CT scan, MRI where available), endoscopy and specialized investigations; (e) to operate intensive care units, high dependency units and specialized care units; (f) to provide inpatient services with large bed capacity across medical, surgical, pediatric, obstetric and gynecological wards; (g) to offer specialist outpatient clinics in various medical and surgical specialties; (h) to provide comprehensive maternal and child health services including antenatal care, safe delivery services, postnatal care and pediatric care; (i) to operate modern theater complex for surgical procedures across specialties; (j) to provide clinical training for undergraduate medical students through affiliation with medical colleges; (k) to conduct postgraduate medical training including residency programmes in multiple specialties; (l) to provide internship training for medical doctors, pharmacists, medical laboratory scientists, nurses and allied health professionals; (m) to offer continuing medical education and professional development; (n) to conduct clinical and health systems research; (o) to maintain comprehensive pharmacy services; (p) to provide physiotherapy, occupational therapy and rehabilitation services; (q) to establish robust disease surveillance, infection control and quality assurance systems; (r) to provide public health services including health education and community health programmes; (s) to respond to disease outbreaks and public health emergencies; and (t) to collaborate with teaching hospitals, specialist hospitals and international health organizations.

## 242. FEDERAL STAFF CLINICS, ABUJA PHASE I

**MDA Code:** 521028001

Federal Staff Clinics, Abuja Phase I, is a federal government health facility providing primary and secondary healthcare services exclusively for federal civil servants and their dependents in Abuja. Part of Federal Staff Hospital system, the clinic offers outpatient services, general consultations, minor procedures, laboratory services and pharmacy services. Located in Phase I area of Abuja, the clinic ensures federal employees have accessible healthcare reducing work absenteeism. Operating under Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare (through Federal Staff Hospital management), the clinic complements larger Federal Staff Hospitals by providing decentralized healthcare access.

**Core Mandate:** To provide quality primary and secondary healthcare services to federal civil servants and their families ensuring healthy workforce.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide outpatient medical consultations for federal staff and dependents
(b) Offer general practitioner services for common illnesses
(c) Provide specialist consultations in internal medicine, pediatrics, obstetrics and gynecology
(d) Conduct diagnostic services including laboratory tests and medical imaging
(e) Provide pharmacy services dispensing medications to patients
(f) Offer minor surgical procedures and wound care
(g) Provide maternal and child health services including antenatal care
(h) Conduct immunization programmes for children of federal staff
(9) Provide family planning counseling and services
(j) Offer health screening and medical check-ups for federal employees
(k) Conduct occupational health services for workplace wellness
(l) Provide treatment for chronic diseases including diabetes and hypertension
(m) Offer emergency first aid and stabilization before referral
(n) Conduct health education and disease prevention programmes
(o) Provide physiotherapy and rehabilitation services
(p) Maintain patient medical records and electronic health systems
(q) Refer complex cases to Federal Staff Hospital or teaching hospitals
(r) Conduct pre-employment medical examinations for federal recruits
(s) Provide sick leave certification for federal employees
(t) Ensure federal staff healthcare under public service conditions of service

Operating under Federal Ministry of Health and Social Welfare, the clinic ensures federal employees maintain optimal eye health.

**Core Mandate:** To provide comprehensive ophthalmology and eye care services to federal civil servants and their families ensuring optimal visual health.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct comprehensive eye examinations for federal staff and dependents
(b) Provide vision testing and visual acuity assessment
(c) Conduct refraction and prescribe corrective lenses (eyeglasses and contact lenses)
(d) Provide diagnosis and treatment of eye diseases including glaucoma, cataracts and diabetic retinopathy
(e) Offer treatment for eye infections including conjunctivitis and keratitis
(f) Conduct diagnosis and management of refractive errors (myopia, hyperopia, astigmatism)
(g) Provide treatment for dry eye syndrome and allergic eye conditions
(h) Offer diagnosis and treatment of retinal diseases
(i) Conduct minor eye surgeries including chalazion removal and pterygium excision
(j) Provide pre-operative assessment for cataract surgery and arrange referrals
(k) Offer pediatric eye care including vision screening for children
(l) Conduct diagnosis and management of amblyopia (lazy eye) and strabismus (squint)
(m) Provide glaucoma screening, diagnosis and management
(n) Offer diabetic eye disease screening for federal staff with diabetes
(o) Conduct hypertensive retinopathy screening and management
(p) Provide low vision assessment and visual aids
(q) Conduct occupational eye health services for computer users
(r) Provide emergency eye care for eye injuries and foreign bodies
(s) Maintain ophthalmology equipment including slit lamps and fundoscopy
(t) Refer complex cases requiring advanced ophthalmology surgery to eye hospitals

(l) Conduct oral health education and preventive counseling
(m) Provide emergency dental care for dental pain and trauma
(n) Offer cosmetic dentistry services where available
(o) Conduct oral cancer screening and early detection
(p) Provide treatment for temporomandibular joint (TMJ) disorders
(q) Maintain modern dental equipment and sterilization standards
(r) Refer complex cases to dental specialists or teaching hospitals

## 63. DENTAL THERAPISTS REGISTRATION BOARD OF NIGERIA
**MDA Code:** 521039001

The Dental Therapists Registration Board of Nigeria (DThRBN) was established by Decree 81 of August 25, 1993, now embodied in the Dental Therapists (Registration, etc.) Act CAP D7 LFN 2004. The Board is a parastatal of the Federal Ministry of Health charged with regulating the training and professional practice of Dental Therapists and allied cadres including Dental Surgery Technicians and Dental Surgery Assistants. The profession plays a crucial role in promoting healthy oral hygiene in Nigeria.

The Board's statutory mandate includes determining what standards of knowledge and skill are to be attained by persons seeking to become members of the dental therapy profession and improving those standards from time to time; securing the establishment and maintenance of a register of persons registered under the Act as members of the profession and publishing lists of those persons; conducting examinations in the profession and awarding certificates or diplomas to successful candidates; prescribing fees for examinations and registration; and performing other functions conferred by the Act.

The Board's key functions include registering and issuing valid renewable licenses to Dental Therapists, Dental Surgery Technicians, and Dental Surgery Assistants; inspecting and monitoring training and practice of professionals; approving and accrediting training programmes and institutions; ensuring professionals operate within the confines of the registration act and comply with professional ethics; and eliminating quackery in dental therapy practice. The Board has established the Dental Therapists Disciplinary Committee to consider and determine cases of professional misconduct referred by the Investigating Panel. The DThRBN works to ensure Nigerians receive quality oral healthcare from properly trained and licensed practitioners while providing leadership in promoting conditions for professionals and enforcing regulations against poor oral hygiene practices.

## 166. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF ENVIRONMENT HEADQUARTERS

**MDA Code:** 535001001

The Federal Ministry of Environment is responsible for environmental protection, conservation, pollution control, climate change mitigation and adaptation, and sustainable environmental management. Established to address environmental challenges including deforestation, desertification, erosion, pollution, biodiversity loss and climate change impacts. Led by Minister of Environment assisted by Minister of State and Permanent Secretary. The Ministry supervises National Environmental Standards and Regulations Enforcement Agency (NESREA), Forestry Research Institute of Nigeria (FRIN), National Oil Spills Detection and Response Agency (NOSDRA), Great Green Wall Agency, Nigerian Conservation Foundation, and coordinates Nigeria's implementation of international environmental conventions including UNFCCC, Convention on Biological Diversity, Montreal Protocol and Ramsar Convention.

**Core Mandate:** To facilitate sustainable management of Nigeria's environment and natural resources for present and future generations, addressing environmental degradation and climate change.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate and implement national environmental policies including National Environmental Policy
(b) Coordinate environmental protection and conservation programmes
(c) Enforce environmental laws and regulations through NESREA
(d) Conduct environmental impact assessments for major development projects
(e) Coordinate climate change mitigation and adaptation strategies through Climate Change Department
(f) Implement Nigeria's Nationally Determined Contributions under Paris Agreement
(g) Coordinate desertification control and Great Green Wall initiative in northern Nigeria
(h) Manage erosion control and watershed management programmes
(i) Coordinate forest resources management and conservation through FRIN
(j) Promote afforestation and reforestation programmes
(k) Manage biodiversity conservation and protected areas
(l) Coordinate pollution control (air, water, soil) and waste management
(m) Regulate and respond to oil spills through NOSDRA
(n) Coordinate environmental emergencies and disasters response
(o) Promote renewable energy and clean energy technologies
(p) Manage Nigeria's obligations under international environmental conventions
(q) Conduct environmental monitoring and reporting
(r) Promote environmental education and public awareness
(s) Regulate pesticides and industrial chemicals
(t) Facilitate green economy initiatives and sustainable production patterns

## 127. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF WILDLIFE MANAGEMENT - NEW BUSSA

**MDA Code:** 535010001

The Federal College of Wildlife Management, New Bussa, is a specialized federal tertiary institution established in 1989 and located in New Bussa, Niger State, along Awuru Road, Kainji-Mokwa Expressway. Operating under the Forestry Research Institute of Nigeria (FRIN) supervised by Federal Ministry of Environment, the college is accredited by National Board for Technical Education (NBTE) to offer National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) programmes in wildlife management, conservation and ecotourism. Located near Kainji Lake and Kainji Lake National Park, the college benefits from proximity to Nigeria's premier wildlife conservation areas and National Institute for Freshwater Fisheries Research, providing unique opportunities for practical wildlife management training. The college's core mandate is to provide specialized education and training in wildlife management and conservation, producing middle-level manpower capable of managing Nigeria's wildlife resources sustainably. Specific functions include: (a) to provide specialized training in wildlife management through National Diploma and Higher National Diploma programmes; (b) to train technicians and technologists in wildlife biology, ecology and behavior for effective species management; (c) to provide education in habitat management, restoration techniques and conservation biology; (d) to train personnel in wildlife monitoring, census techniques, population assessment and research methodologies; (e) to educate students in captive animal care, zoo management and ex-situ conservation; (f) to provide training in protected area management, national park administration and wildlife reserve operations; (g) to train personnel in wildlife law enforcement, anti-poaching strategies and community conservation; (h) to educate students in ecotourism development, interpretation and visitor management; (i) to provide training in human-wildlife conflict management and mitigation strategies; (j) to train technologists in wildlife health management, disease surveillance and veterinary care for wild animals; (k) to educate students in biodiversity conservation, endangered species management and recovery programmes; (l) to provide training in community-based wildlife management, participatory conservation and stakeholder engagement; (m) to train personnel in environmental education, conservation awareness and public outreach; (n) to conduct research in wildlife management, conservation biology and ecosystem management; (o) to collaborate with local communities, government agencies, non-governmental organizations and stakeholders to promote wildlife conservation and sustainable development; and (p) to ensure better education for better forest and wildlife conservation.

## 118. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF FORESTRY IBADAN

**MDA Code:** 535011001

The Federal College of Forestry Ibadan is a pioneer forestry education institution in Nigeria operated by the Forestry Research Institute of Nigeria (FRIN) under Federal Ministry of Environment. Located at Jericho, Ibadan, Oyo State, the college operates as a monotechnic institution approved by National Board for Technical Education (NBTE) offering National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) in Forestry Technology and related disciplines. FRIN was established in 1954 as Federal Department of Forestry Research, with status changed to an institute in 1977, retaining aspects of forestry research and education as federal responsibility even after regional administrative divisions. The college's core mandate is to produce technical manpower effecting sustainable development of forestry in Nigeria and Africa, and to serve as credible reference in all matters of forestry education, training and research. Specific functions include: (a) to train technicians and technologists for agricultural, wood, forest and environmental services through full-time and part-time ND and HND programmes; (b) to provide education in forestry technology, forest management and silviculture; (c) to train personnel in wood science, timber technology and forest products utilization; (d) to provide training in forest nursery management, tree breeding and seed technology; (e) to educate students in agroforestry, plantation forestry and social forestry; (f) to train personnel in wildlife management, ecotourism and biodiversity conservation; (g) to provide training in forest protection, pest management and fire ecology; (h) to educate students in forest mensuration, inventory and remote sensing applications; (i) to train personnel in forest economics, policy and community forestry; (j) to conduct forestry research and development; (k) to produce men and women with scientific knowledge necessary to rebuild threatened forest resources through appropriate management technology; (l) to maintain curriculum designed to foster intellectual and practical growth; (m) to provide extensive laboratory and field practical training; and (n) to offer safe, nurturing educational environment with dedicated lecturers and extracurricular activities supporting well-rounded education. The college maintains admission requirements of JAMB UTME minimum score of 100 for ND programmes.

## 119. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF FORESTRY JOS

**MDA Code:** 535012001

The Federal College of Forestry Jos is a federal tertiary institution operated by Forestry Research Institute of Nigeria (FRIN) under Federal Ministry of Environment, located in Jos, Plateau State. Operating as a monotechnic institution approved by National Board for Technical Education (NBTE), the college offers National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) programmes in forestry technology and environmental related disciplines. The college serves as the forestry education institution for the North-Central geo-political zone and Middle Belt region, with particular focus on montane, guinea savannah and plateau ecosystem management. The college's core mandate is to produce men and women with scientific knowledge necessary to rebuild the population of threatened forest resources of the country through appropriate management technology, and to train technicians and technologists for agricultural, wood, forest and environmental services. Specific functions include: (a) to provide specialized training in forestry technology through National Diploma and Higher National Diploma programmes; (b) to train forestry technicians and technologists in sustainable forest management appropriate for plateau and savannah ecosystems; (c) to provide education and training in environmental management, watershed management and soil conservation for highland areas; (d) to train personnel in forest resource assessment, inventory and monitoring for diverse ecosystems; (e) to provide training in agroforestry systems, plantation forestry and farm forestry suitable for Middle Belt region; (f) to educate students in wildlife management, rangeland management and biodiversity conservation in savannah and montane ecosystems; (g) to train personnel in wood science, timber technology and forest products processing; (h) to provide training in forest nursery management, tree breeding and selection of species suitable for highland and savannah zones; (i) to educate students in forest protection, fire management and pest control; (j) to train personnel in community forestry, extension services and participatory forest management; (k) to conduct applied research in forestry and environmental management for plateau and Middle Belt ecosystems; (l) to provide training in land degradation control, erosion management and environmental restoration; and (m) to award certificates preparing graduates for National Youth Service Corps mobilization and professional forestry careers.

## 117. FEDERAL COLLEGE OF FOREST RESOURCES MANAGEMENT, SAKPOBA, EDO STATE (SOUTH-SOUTH)

The Federal College of Forest Resources Management, Fugar (Sakpoba), Edo State, is a federal tertiary institution located in Fugar, Edo State, representing the South-South geo-political zone's forestry education infrastructure. Operating under the auspices of Forestry Research Institute of Nigeria (FRIN) supervised by Federal Ministry of Environment and fully accredited by National Board for Technical Education (NBTE), the college is at the forefront of creating environmental awareness and sustainability in Nigeria. The college's core mandate is to train middle-level manpower in forestry and environmental related disciplines for sustenance and development of forestry and agriculture in Nigeria, with focus on tropical rainforest and humid forest zone management. Specific functions include: (a) to provide specialized training in forestry technology through National Diploma (ND) and Higher National Diploma (HND) programmes in forestry and environmental related disciplines; (b) to train forestry technicians and technologists in sustainable tropical forest management; (c) to provide education and training in environmental management, forest conservation and biodiversity protection in rainforest ecosystems; (d) to train personnel in forest resource assessment, inventory, monitoring and tropical timber management; (e) to provide training in agroforestry suitable for humid zones, forest plantation establishment and management of indigenous tree species; (f) to educate students in wildlife management, biodiversity conservation and protected area management in rainforest ecosystems; (g) to train personnel in wood science, forest products technology, timber processing and non-timber forest product utilization; (h) to provide training in forest nursery management, propagation techniques and tree improvement for tropical species; (i) to educate students in forest protection, pest and disease management relevant to humid tropical environments; (j) to train personnel in community forestry, participatory forest management and forest extension services; (k) to offer wide variety of forestry and environmental related programmes geared toward equipping students in modern technologies and practices; (l) to conduct applied research in forestry and environmental management for tropical forest ecosystems; and (m) to award National Diploma and Higher National Diploma certificates with graduates duly mobilized for National Youth Service Corps.

## 70. ENVIRONMENTAL HEALTH REGISTRATION COUNCIL OF NIGERIA (EHORECON)
**MDA Code:** 535017001

The Environmental Health Officers Registration Council of Nigeria (EHORECON), now also known as the Environmental Health Council of Nigeria (EHCON), was established by the Environmental Health Officers (Registration, etc.) Act 11 of 2002 (as amended on August 6, 2022) to regulate the environmental health profession in Nigeria. Environmental health remained unregulated in Nigeria until 2002 when the government granted it professional recognition. The Council operates under the Federal Ministry of Environment and was first inaugurated in March 2004.

The Council's statutory mandate includes determining what standards of knowledge and skill are to be attained by persons seeking to become members of the profession of Environmental Health and improving those standards as circumstances may permit; securing the establishment and maintenance of a register of persons registered under the Act as members of the profession and publishing lists of those persons; conducting examinations in the profession and awarding certificates or diplomas to successful candidates; establishing and reviewing a code of conduct for effective practice of the Environmental Health profession; and performing other functions conferred by the Act.

EHORECON's key functions include registering Environmental Health Officers, Environmental Health Technicians, and Environmental Health Assistants; issuing renewable practice licenses valid for two years; accrediting training programmes and institutions offering environmental health courses at National Diploma, Higher National Diploma, and degree levels; inspecting and monitoring professional practice; enforcing professional standards and ethics; and eliminating quackery in environmental health practice. The Council has established an Environmental Health Disciplinary Committee and Investigating Panel to handle cases of professional misconduct. EHORECON works to ensure that environmental health professionals are equipped with investigative, analytical, communicative, educative, organizational, and attitudinal skills necessary for effective practice in areas including sanitation, food hygiene, disease control, and environmental standards enforcement.

---

## 170. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF HUMANITARIAN AFFAIRS AND POVERTY ALLEVIATION

**MDA Code:** 554001001

The Federal Ministry of Humanitarian Affairs and Poverty Alleviation (established 2019 as Federal Ministry of Humanitarian Affairs, Disaster Management and Social Development, renamed to emphasize poverty alleviation, later merged with Health Ministry in October 2024 but previously operational as standalone) was responsible for humanitarian response, disaster management, internally displaced persons assistance, refugees management, social development and poverty alleviation programmes. The Ministry coordinated Nigeria's humanitarian interventions particularly in North-East affected by Boko Haram insurgency and implemented National Social Investment Programmes. When operational, it supervised National Emergency Management Agency (NEMA), National Commission for Refugees, Migrants and Internally Displaced Persons, and managed social investment programmes including N-Power, Conditional Cash Transfer, Government Enterprise and Empowerment Programme (GEEP), and Home-Grown School Feeding.

**Core Mandate (when operational):** To coordinate humanitarian assistance, manage disaster response, protect vulnerable populations and implement poverty alleviation interventions.

**Statutory Functions (when operational):**
(a) Coordinate humanitarian response for conflict-affected and disaster-affected populations
(b) Oversee National Emergency Management Agency for disaster preparedness and response
(c) Coordinate assistance for internally displaced persons (IDPs) in camps and host communities
(d) Manage refugees and migrants through National Commission for Refugees
(e) Implement National Social Investment Programmes including N-Power and Conditional Cash Transfer
(f) Coordinate poverty alleviation interventions targeting extreme poor and vulnerable
(g) Implement social protection programmes and safety nets
(h) Manage humanitarian response in North-East Nigeria
(i) Coordinate reconstruction and rehabilitation in conflict-affected areas
(j) Protect vulnerable groups including children, women, elderly and persons with disabilities
(k) Coordinate with international humanitarian organizations and UN agencies
(l) Manage humanitarian aid distribution and relief materials
(m) Implement livelihood support programmes for vulnerable populations
(n) Coordinate psychosocial support for trauma-affected populations
(o) Manage social registers of poor and vulnerable households
(p) Implement school feeding programmes nationwide
(q) Coordinate youth empowerment and job creation schemes through N-Power
(r) Manage post-disaster recovery and resilience building
(s) Coordinate disaster risk reduction and early warning systems

---

## 188. FEDERAL MINISTRY OF YOUTH DEVELOPMENT - HQTRS

**MDA Code:** 555001001

The Federal Ministry of Youth Development (separated from Sports in 2019, previously combined as Ministry of Youth and Sports Development) is responsible for youth policy formulation, youth empowerment, youth skills acquisition and youth development programmes. The Ministry coordinates National Youth Service Corps (NYSC) in partnership with Ministry of Special Duties, implements youth employment schemes, and promotes youth entrepreneurship. With over 60% of Nigeria's population under 25 years, youth development is strategic for harnessing demographic dividend.

**Core Mandate:** To formulate and implement policies for youth development, empowerment and engagement, harnessing youth potential for national development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Formulate and implement National Youth Policy and youth development strategies
(b) Coordinate National Youth Service Corps (NYSC) programme administration
(c) Promote youth empowerment through skills acquisition and vocational training
(d) Coordinate youth employment programmes and job creation initiatives
(e) Facilitate youth entrepreneurship and access to startup capital
(f) Promote youth participation in governance and decision-making
(g) Coordinate youth leadership development and civic education programmes
(h) Combat youth restiveness and violent extremism through engagement programmes
(i) Promote youth volunteerism and community service
(j) Coordinate youth sports development and recreation facilities
(k) Facilitate youth access to education including scholarship programmes
(l) Promote youth innovation and technology adoption
(m) Coordinate youth cooperatives and youth self-help groups
(n) Combat drug abuse among youth through prevention programmes
(o) Promote youth mental health and psychosocial support
(p) Coordinate youth exchange programmes and international youth engagement
(q) Establish and maintain youth development centers nationwide
(r) Facilitate youth participation in agriculture and agribusiness
(s) Promote gender equality and inclusion in youth programmes
(t) Coordinate youth policy implementation across ministries and states
